```go
// SceneAudio holds the complete blocks of one scene.
type SceneAudio struct {
	Blocks []AudioBlock `json:"blocks"`
}

// AudioInfo is the compile input. Music is never in it.
type AudioInfo struct {
	Narration map[string]SceneAudio `json:"narration"`
}

// MissingBlock names a block with at least one piece lacking audio or a valid envelope.
type MissingBlock struct {
	SceneID string `json:"scene_id"`
	BlockID string `json:"block_id"`
	Chars   int    `json:"chars"` // code points of the missing pieces' text
}

// CharCounts counts code points of piece text, which is what the cloud caps count.
type CharCounts struct {
	Total   int `json:"total"`
	Missing int `json:"missing"`
}

// MusicRef names the stored music of a story.
type MusicRef struct {
	Key        string `json:"key"`
	Ext        string `json:"ext"`
	DurationMS int    `json:"duration_ms"`
}

// AudioAnswer is the body of GET /projects/{id}/narration.
type AudioAnswer struct {
	Narration  map[string]SceneAudio `json:"narration"`
	Missing    []MissingBlock        `json:"missing"`
	Chars      CharCounts            `json:"chars"`
	Music      *MusicRef             `json:"music"`
	MusicIssue string                `json:"music_issue,omitempty"` // "source_missing"
}

// Info returns the part of the answer that compile reads.
func (a AudioAnswer) Info() AudioInfo { return AudioInfo{Narration: a.Narration} }

// ErrMusicSourceMissing reports a file or track source that no longer exists.
var ErrMusicSourceMissing = errors.New("storymaker: the music source is missing")

// MusicKeyResolver returns the key of the story's music. "" means nothing is to be played: mode
// none, no music, volume 0, or mode file with no source chosen yet. A nil resolver means "". A
// file or track source that was set and no longer exists returns ("", ErrMusicSourceMissing).
type MusicKeyResolver func(ctx context.Context, m *Music) (string, error)

// PieceFile is what PieceFiles found for one piece. HasEnv says the file exists; ParseEnvelope
// decides validity.
type PieceFile struct {
	Ext    string
	HasEnv bool
}

var (
	// storedPieceName is the whitelist of PieceFiles. It also skips leftover .aurago-write-* files.
	storedPieceName = regexp.MustCompile(`^([0-9a-f]{64})\.(wav|mp3|env\.json)$`)
	// storedAudioName is the whitelist of AudioPath and OpenAudio. The audio route has its own, narrower one.
	storedAudioName = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{64}\.(wav|mp3|env\.json|meta\.json|pending\.json)$`)
)

// AudioAnswer builds the answer for a decoded story. A block enters Narration only when every piece
// has audio and a valid envelope; otherwise it enters Missing and JS estimates it. A missing music
// source sets MusicIssue and keeps the narration; any other resolver error is returned.
func (p *ProjectStore) AudioAnswer(ctx context.Context, id string, s *Story, resolve MusicKeyResolver) (AudioAnswer, error) {
	files, err := p.PieceFiles(ctx, id)
	if err != nil {
		return AudioAnswer{}, err
	}
	ans := AudioAnswer{Narration: map[string]SceneAudio{}, Missing: []MissingBlock{}}
	for _, b := range Blocks(s) {
		pieces := PiecesOf(b)
		inputs := make([]AudioPieceInput, 0, len(pieces))
		missing := 0
		for _, piece := range pieces {
			chars := runeLen(piece.Text)
			ans.Chars.Total += chars
			in, ok, err := p.storedPiece(ctx, id, b.Voice, piece, files)
			if err != nil {
				return AudioAnswer{}, err
			}
			if !ok {
				missing += chars
				continue
			}
			inputs = append(inputs, in)
		}
		if missing > 0 {
			ans.Chars.Missing += missing
			ans.Missing = append(ans.Missing, MissingBlock{SceneID: b.SceneID, BlockID: b.ID, Chars: missing})
			continue
		}
		scene := ans.Narration[b.SceneID]
		scene.Blocks = append(scene.Blocks, AssembleBlock(b, inputs, s.Format.FPS))
		ans.Narration[b.SceneID] = scene
	}
	// Only the resolver's error counts here, so a key leaves Music null. Batch 2C wraps this body with
	// the lookup of the key's audio and meta files.
	if resolve != nil {
		if _, err := resolve(ctx, s.Music); err != nil {
			if !errors.Is(err, ErrMusicSourceMissing) {
				return AudioAnswer{}, fmt.Errorf("storymaker: music key: %w", err)
			}
			ans.MusicIssue = "source_missing"
		}
	}
	return ans, nil
}

// storedPiece returns the input of a piece whose audio and valid envelope exist. Envelopes are
// ordinary workspace files, so an unreadable or invalid one counts as missing.
func (p *ProjectStore) storedPiece(ctx context.Context, id string, v Voice, piece Piece, files map[string]PieceFile) (AudioPieceInput, bool, error) {
	hash := PieceKey(v, piece.Text)
	f := files[hash]
	if f.Ext == "" || !f.HasEnv {
		return AudioPieceInput{}, false, nil
	}
	data, _, err := p.st.ReadFile(ctx, p.file(id, "audio", "narration", hash+".env.json"))
	if err != nil {
		if ctx.Err() != nil {
			return AudioPieceInput{}, false, ctx.Err()
		}
		return AudioPieceInput{}, false, nil
	}
	env, err := ParseEnvelope(data)
	if err != nil {
		return AudioPieceInput{}, false, nil
	}
	return AudioPieceInput{Hash: hash, Ext: f.Ext, Chars: piece.Chars, Join: piece.Join, Env: env}, true, nil
}

// PieceFiles lists audio/narration and accepts only names matching storedPieceName. When a hash
// has both formats the first in sorted order wins.
func (p *ProjectStore) PieceFiles(ctx context.Context, id string) (map[string]PieceFile, error) {
	if err := p.checkID(id); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	names, err := p.st.List(ctx, p.file(id, "audio", "narration"))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("storymaker: list narration audio: %w", err)
	}
	out := make(map[string]PieceFile, len(names))
	for _, name := range names {
		m := storedPieceName.FindStringSubmatch(name)
		if m == nil {
			continue
		}
		f := out[m[1]]
		if m[2] == "env.json" {
			f.HasEnv = true
		} else if f.Ext == "" {
			f.Ext = m[2]
		}
		out[m[1]] = f
	}
	return out, nil
}

// AudioPath is the desktop path of an audio file, for Publisher.WriteStream. folder is narration
// or music. The name must match storedAudioName, so it also reaches the envelope and music
// bookkeeping files. The audio route keeps its own whitelist for what it serves.
func (p *ProjectStore) AudioPath(id, folder, file string) (string, error) {
	if err := p.checkID(id); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	if folder != "narration" && folder != "music" {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: audio folder %q", ErrInvalidArgument, clip(folder, 40))
	}
	if !storedAudioName.MatchString(file) {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: audio file %q", ErrInvalidArgument, clip(file, 80))
	}
	return p.file(id, "audio", folder, file), nil
}

// OpenAudio opens a file named by AudioPath. A missing file wraps fs.ErrNotExist.
func (p *ProjectStore) OpenAudio(ctx context.Context, id, folder, file string) (io.ReadSeekCloser, FileInfo, error) {
	full, err := p.AudioPath(id, folder, file)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, FileInfo{}, err
	}
	return p.st.Open(ctx, full)
}

// WritePiece stores the audio create-only, so an existing file is not an error and stays, then
// writes the envelope.
func (p *ProjectStore) WritePiece(ctx context.Context, id, hash, ext string, data []byte, env Envelope) error {
	full, err := p.pieceAudioPath(id, hash, ext)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if _, err := p.st.WriteFile(ctx, full, data, MustNotExist); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, ErrConflict) {
		return fmt.Errorf("storymaker: write narration audio: %w", err)
	}
	return p.WriteEnvelope(ctx, id, hash, env)
}

// WriteEnvelope writes or replaces the envelope of a stored piece.
func (p *ProjectStore) WriteEnvelope(ctx context.Context, id, hash string, env Envelope) error {
	full, err := p.AudioPath(id, "narration", hash+".env.json")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	data, err := json.Marshal(env)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("storymaker: encode envelope: %w", err)
	}
	if _, err := p.st.WriteFile(ctx, full, data, ""); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("storymaker: write envelope: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

// RemovePiece removes the audio and the envelope of a piece. A missing file is not an error.
func (p *ProjectStore) RemovePiece(ctx context.Context, id, hash, ext string) error {
	full, err := p.pieceAudioPath(id, hash, ext)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := p.st.Remove(ctx, full); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("storymaker: remove narration audio: %w", err)
	}
	env, err := p.AudioPath(id, "narration", hash+".env.json")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := p.st.Remove(ctx, env); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("storymaker: remove envelope: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

func (p *ProjectStore) pieceAudioPath(id, hash, ext string) (string, error) {
	if ext != "wav" && ext != "mp3" {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: audio format %q", ErrInvalidArgument, clip(ext, 16))
	}
	return p.AudioPath(id, "narration", hash+"."+ext)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

Create `internal/storymaker/voices.go`:

```go
package storymaker

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"sort"
	"strings"
)

// VoiceOption is one voice of a provider.
type VoiceOption struct {
	ID        string `json:"id"`
	Name      string `json:"name,omitempty"`
	Language  string `json:"language,omitempty"`
	Gender    string `json:"gender,omitempty"`
	Installed *bool  `json:"installed,omitempty"`
	Note      string `json:"note,omitempty"`
}

// VoiceProvider is one entry of GET /voices.
type VoiceProvider struct {
	ID              string        `json:"id"`
	Active          bool          `json:"active"`
	Ready           bool          `json:"ready"`
	Voices          []VoiceOption `json:"voices,omitempty"`
	FreeText        bool          `json:"free_text,omitempty"`
	Languages       []string      `json:"languages"`        // "*" means not restricted
	TestedLanguages []string      `json:"tested_languages"` // never null; empty in phase 2
	NeedsInstall    bool          `json:"needs_install"`
	DefaultVoice    string        `json:"default_voice,omitempty"`
	ChunkLimit      int           `json:"chunk_limit"`
	Note            string        `json:"note,omitempty"`
}

// ErrNoVoiceForLanguage reports that no ready provider speaks the language.
var ErrNoVoiceForLanguage = errors.New("storymaker: no voice for this language")

// ResolveProjectVoice picks the voice of a new project. providers has the order of
// storymakerVoiceProviders: the active provider, then local ones, then cloud ones.
//  1. the active provider, if ready and it supports the language, with its configured voice
//     (DefaultVoice) when that voice speaks the language
//  2. else that provider's default voice for the language
//  3. else the first other ready provider in list order that supports the language
//
// When nothing fits it returns ErrNoVoiceForLanguage and the languages ready providers speak.
func ResolveProjectVoice(providers []VoiceProvider, language string, defaultVoiceFor func(provider, language string) string) (Voice, []string, error) {
	language = strings.TrimSpace(language)
	base := LanguageBase(language)
	for i := range providers {
		p := &providers[i]
		if !p.Active || !p.Ready || !providerSpeaks(p, base) {
			continue
		}
		id := NormalizeProvider(p.ID)
		voice := p.DefaultVoice
		if !voiceSpeaks(p, voice, base) {
			voice = defaultVoiceOf(p, id, language, base, defaultVoiceFor)
		}
		return Voice{Provider: id, Voice: voice, Language: language}, nil, nil
	}
	for i := range providers {
		p := &providers[i]
		if p.Active || !p.Ready || !providerSpeaks(p, base) {
			continue
		}
		id := NormalizeProvider(p.ID)
		return Voice{Provider: id, Voice: defaultVoiceOf(p, id, language, base, defaultVoiceFor), Language: language}, nil, nil
	}
	return Voice{}, readyLanguages(providers), ErrNoVoiceForLanguage
}

func providerSpeaks(p *VoiceProvider, base string) bool {
	for _, l := range p.Languages {
		if l == "*" || LanguageBase(l) == base {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

// voiceSpeaks says whether a voice id speaks the language. A provider without a list has nothing
// to check against, so its id counts. A listed voice without a language counts too.
func voiceSpeaks(p *VoiceProvider, voice, base string) bool {
	if voice == "" {
		return false
	}
	for _, o := range p.Voices {
		if o.ID == voice {
			return o.Language == "" || LanguageBase(o.Language) == base
		}
	}
	return len(p.Voices) == 0
}

// defaultVoiceOf asks defaultVoiceFor first. Without an answer it takes the provider's own voice,
// then its first installed voice of that language, so a list provider does not run with an empty
// voice that would mean the configured voice of another language.
func defaultVoiceOf(p *VoiceProvider, id, language, base string, defaultVoiceFor func(provider, language string) string) string {
	if defaultVoiceFor != nil {
		if v := defaultVoiceFor(id, language); v != "" {
			return v
		}
	}
	if voiceSpeaks(p, p.DefaultVoice, base) {
		return p.DefaultVoice
	}
	for _, o := range p.Voices {
		if LanguageBase(o.Language) == base && (o.Installed == nil || *o.Installed) {
			return o.ID
		}
	}
	return ""
}

// readyLanguages is the sorted union of what ready providers speak, without "*".
func readyLanguages(providers []VoiceProvider) []string {
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	out := []string{}
	for i := range providers {
		if !providers[i].Ready {
			continue
		}
		for _, l := range providers[i].Languages {
			base := LanguageBase(l)
			if l == "*" || base == "" || seen[base] {
				continue
			}
			seen[base] = true
			out = append(out, base)
		}
	}
	sort.Strings(out)
	return out
}

// LanguageBase is the first segment of the lower-cased tag: "pt-BR" and "pt_BR" give "pt".
func LanguageBase(tag string) string {
	tag = strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(tag))
	if i := strings.IndexAny(tag, "-_"); i >= 0 {
		return tag[:i]
	}
	return tag
}

// LocalVoiceProvider says whether a provider runs on this box. Local providers are exempt from the
// character caps. It normalises its argument.
func LocalVoiceProvider(id string) bool {
	switch NormalizeProvider(id) {
	case "phonon", "piper", "sanotts", "supertonic", "speech_lab":
		return true
	}
	return false
}

// ApplyCaptionOverride sets style.captions when burnIn is not nil.
func ApplyCaptionOverride(s *Story, burnIn *bool) {
	if burnIn != nil {
		s.Style.Captions = *burnIn
	}
}

// CreateWithVoice is Create with story.voice set to a copy of voice. A nil voice leaves voice:null.
func (p *ProjectStore) CreateWithVoice(ctx context.Context, title, aspect string, fps int, voice *Voice) (Project, Story, string, error) {
	title = strings.TrimSpace(title)
	if title == "" || runeLen(title) > 120 {
		return Project{}, Story{}, "", fmt.Errorf("%w: the title must have 1 to 120 characters", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
	if aspect == "" {
		aspect = DefaultAspect
	}
	if aspect != "16:9" && aspect != "9:16" && aspect != "1:1" {
		return Project{}, Story{}, "", fmt.Errorf("%w: aspect must be 16:9, 9:16 or 1:1", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
	if fps == 0 {
		fps = DefaultFPS
	}
	if fps != 24 && fps != 25 && fps != 30 {
		return Project{}, Story{}, "", fmt.Errorf("%w: fps must be 24, 25 or 30", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}

	id := p.newID()
	now := p.now()
	story := Story{
		Schema: Schema, Title: title,
		Format: Format{Aspect: aspect, FPS: fps},
		Style:  Style{Palette: DefaultPalette, Font: DefaultFont, Captions: true},
		Cast:   []CastMember{},
		Scenes: []Scene{{ID: "s1"}},
	}
	if voice != nil {
		v := *voice
		story.Voice = &v
	}
	ApplyDefaults(&story)
	storyBytes, err := CanonicalJSON(story)
	if err != nil {
		return Project{}, Story{}, "", err
	}
	proj := Project{ID: id, Title: title, Aspect: aspect, FPS: fps, CreatedAt: now, UpdatedAt: now}
	projBytes, err := encodeProject(proj)
	if err != nil {
		return Project{}, Story{}, "", err
	}

	for _, sub := range []string{"", "assets", "audio/narration", "audio/music", "exports"} {
		if err := p.st.MkdirAll(ctx, p.file(id, sub)); err != nil {
			p.cleanup(id)
			return Project{}, Story{}, "", fmt.Errorf("storymaker: create project folders: %w", err)
		}
	}
	etag, err := p.st.WriteFile(ctx, p.file(id, "story.json"), storyBytes, MustNotExist)
	if err == nil {
		_, err = p.st.WriteFile(ctx, p.file(id, "project.json"), projBytes, MustNotExist)
	}
	if err != nil {
		p.cleanup(id)
		return Project{}, Story{}, "", fmt.Errorf("storymaker: create project: %w", err)
	}
	return proj, story, etag, nil
}
```

```go
func (p *ProjectStore) Create(ctx context.Context, title, aspect string, fps int) (Project, Story, string, error) {
	title = strings.TrimSpace(title)
	if title == "" || runeLen(title) > 120 {
		return Project{}, Story{}, "", fmt.Errorf("%w: the title must have 1 to 120 characters", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
	if aspect == "" {
		aspect = DefaultAspect
	}
	if aspect != "16:9" && aspect != "9:16" && aspect != "1:1" {
		return Project{}, Story{}, "", fmt.Errorf("%w: aspect must be 16:9, 9:16 or 1:1", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}
	if fps == 0 {
		fps = DefaultFPS
	}
	if fps != 24 && fps != 25 && fps != 30 {
		return Project{}, Story{}, "", fmt.Errorf("%w: fps must be 24, 25 or 30", ErrInvalidArgument)
	}

	id := p.newID()
	now := p.now()
	story := Story{
		Schema: Schema, Title: title,
		Format: Format{Aspect: aspect, FPS: fps},
		Style:  Style{Palette: DefaultPalette, Font: DefaultFont, Captions: true},
		Cast:   []CastMember{},
		Scenes: []Scene{{ID: "s1"}},
	}
	ApplyDefaults(&story)
	storyBytes, err := CanonicalJSON(story)
	if err != nil {
		return Project{}, Story{}, "", err
	}
	proj := Project{ID: id, Title: title, Aspect: aspect, FPS: fps, CreatedAt: now, UpdatedAt: now}
	projBytes, err := encodeProject(proj)
	if err != nil {
		return Project{}, Story{}, "", err
	}

	for _, sub := range []string{"", "assets", "audio/narration", "audio/music", "exports"} {
		if err := p.st.MkdirAll(ctx, p.file(id, sub)); err != nil {
			p.cleanup(id)
			return Project{}, Story{}, "", fmt.Errorf("storymaker: create project folders: %w", err)
		}
	}
	etag, err := p.st.WriteFile(ctx, p.file(id, "story.json"), storyBytes, MustNotExist)
	if err == nil {
		_, err = p.st.WriteFile(ctx, p.file(id, "project.json"), projBytes, MustNotExist)
	}
	if err != nil {
		p.cleanup(id)
		return Project{}, Story{}, "", fmt.Errorf("storymaker: create project: %w", err)
	}
	return proj, story, etag, nil
}
```

```go
package tools

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"aurago/internal/testutil"
)

func TestTTSElevenLabsEscapesTheVoiceIDAndSendsRealJSON(t *testing.T) {
	// The quote and the umlaut must survive. U+007F is here because fmt's %q writes it as \x7f,
	// an escape JSON does not have, so the old body could not be parsed.
	text := "Sie sagt \"Grüße\" \x7f"
	type request struct {
		path string
		body []byte
	}
	seen := make(chan request, 1)
	server := testutil.NewHTTPServer(t, http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		body, _ := io.ReadAll(r.Body)
		seen <- request{path: r.URL.EscapedPath(), body: body}
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "audio/mpeg")
		_, _ = w.Write([]byte("ID3fixture-audio"))
	}))
	defer server.Close()
	routeVendorHostTo(t, &ttsHTTPClient, "api.elevenlabs.io", server)

	cfg := TTSConfig{}
	cfg.ElevenLabs.APIKey = "fixture-key"
	cfg.ElevenLabs.VoiceID = "team/voice"
	cfg.ElevenLabs.ModelID = "model-x"
	audio, err := ttsElevenLabs(cfg, text)
	if err != nil || string(audio) != "ID3fixture-audio" {
		t.Fatalf("ttsElevenLabs = %q, %v", audio, err)
	}
	got := <-seen
	// PathEscape writes the slash as %2F, so the id stays one path segment.
	if got.path != "/v1/text-to-speech/team%2Fvoice" {
		t.Errorf("path = %q", got.path)
	}
	var body struct {
		Text          string `json:"text"`
		ModelID       string `json:"model_id"`
		VoiceSettings struct {
			Stability       float64 `json:"stability"`
			SimilarityBoost float64 `json:"similarity_boost"`
		} `json:"voice_settings"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(got.body, &body); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the body is not JSON: %v\n%s", err, got.body)
	}
	if body.Text != text || body.ModelID != "model-x" || body.VoiceSettings.Stability != 0.5 || body.VoiceSettings.SimilarityBoost != 0.75 {
		t.Errorf("body = %+v", body)
	}
}

func TestTTSMiniMaxRefusesABodyOverTheLimit(t *testing.T) {
	// Valid JSON with valid hex, so only the size limit can refuse it. The hex alone fills the limit,
	// and the JSON around it goes past it.
	body := `{"data":{"audio":"` + strings.Repeat("ab", int(maxHTTPResponseSize)/2) + `"},"base_resp":{"status_code":0}}`
	server := testutil.NewHTTPServer(t, http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
		_, _ = w.Write([]byte(body))
	}))
	defer server.Close()
	routeVendorHostTo(t, &ttsHTTPClient, "api.minimax.io", server)

	cfg := TTSConfig{}
	cfg.MiniMax.APIKey = "fixture-key"
	audio, err := ttsMiniMax(cfg, "text")
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "exceeds limit") {
		t.Fatalf("ttsMiniMax = %d bytes, %v; want the size limit error", len(audio), err)
	}
}

func TestOpenAITTSVoicesListsThirteen(t *testing.T) {
	want := []string{"alloy", "ash", "ballad", "coral", "echo", "fable", "nova", "onyx", "sage", "shimmer", "verse", "marin", "cedar"}
	got := OpenAITTSVoices()
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Fatalf("OpenAITTSVoices = %v, want %v", got, want)
	}
	got[0] = "changed"
	if OpenAITTSVoices()[0] != "alloy" {
		t.Fatal("callers share the list")
	}
}
```

This is the limit the other providers use. MiniMax sends hex, so it allows about 5 MiB of MP3, which is over five minutes at the 128 kbit/s the request asks for. A Storymaker piece for MiniMax has at most 400 characters (`ChunkLimit`), so narration stays well under it.

```go
// 999 is one below the floor of max_daily_chars, so the page's PUT is refused before the file changes.
func TestStorymakerConfigRefusesANarrationCapOutsideItsRange(t *testing.T) {
	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "config.yaml")
	original := "server:\n  host: 127.0.0.1\n"
	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(original), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cfg, err := config.Load(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cfg.ConfigPath = path
	s := &Server{Cfg: cfg, Logger: slog.Default()}
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	handleUpdateConfig(s).ServeHTTP(rec, httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPut, "/api/config", strings.NewReader(`{"storymaker":{"narration":{"max_daily_chars":999}}}`)))
	if rec.Code != http.StatusBadRequest || !strings.Contains(rec.Body.String(), "max_daily_chars") {
		t.Fatalf("PUT = %d %s", rec.Code, rec.Body.String())
	}
	stored, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil || string(stored) != original {
		t.Fatalf("a refused patch changed the config file: %v\n%s", err, stored)
	}
}
```

```js
    'Gräns för berättarröst per jobb (tecken)',

    '云端语音达到此字符数后当天停止。本地语音没有上限。范围 1000 到 100000000。',

    '一个旁白任务最多向云端语音发送这么多字符。本地语音没有上限。范围 100 到 10000000。',
  ],
};

for (const [lang, values] of Object.entries(texts)) {
  const file = join('ui', 'lang', 'config', 'common', lang + '.json');
  const source = readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
  const match = source.match(/^ {2}"config\.storymaker\.part_browser": .*,\n/m);
  if (!match) throw new Error(file + ': the part_browser line was not found');
  const added = keys
    .map((key, i) => '  ' + JSON.stringify('config.storymaker.' + key) + ': ' + JSON.stringify(values[i]) + ',\n')
    .join('');
  const end = match.index + match[0].length;
  const next = source.slice(0, end) + added + source.slice(end);
  JSON.parse(next);
  writeFileSync(file, next);
}
```

```go
func noopJobRun(context.Context, Job, any, *Handle) (Output, error) { return Output{}, nil }

func TestEnqueueCoalescesPerProjectAndKind(t *testing.T) {
	release := make(chan struct{})
	defer close(release)
	m, err := NewManager(ManagerOptions{Dir: t.TempDir(), Kinds: []string{KindNarrate, KindMusic}, Run: func(ctx context.Context, job Job, _ any, h *Handle) (Output, error) {
		select {
		case <-release:
		case <-ctx.Done():
		}
		return Output{}, nil
	}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(m.Close)

	// Eight racing calls make one job, and seven of them are told it is a replay.
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	var replays atomic.Int32
	ids := make([]string, 8)
	for i := range ids {
		wg.Add(1)
		go func(i int) {
			defer wg.Done()
			job, replayed, err := m.Enqueue(EnqueueRequest{ProjectID: projA, Coalesce: true})
			if err != nil {
				t.Error(err)
				return
			}
			ids[i] = job.ID
			if replayed {
				replays.Add(1)
			}
		}(i)
	}
	wg.Wait()
	if replays.Load() != 7 {
		t.Fatalf("%d replays, want 7", replays.Load())
	}
	for _, id := range ids {
		if id != ids[0] {
			t.Fatalf("two jobs were created: %v", ids)
		}
	}
	if first, _ := m.Get(ids[0]); first.Kind != KindNarrate {
		t.Fatalf("an empty kind gave %q, want the first of Kinds", first.Kind)
	}

	// Another kind and another project each get their own job.
	music, replayed, err := m.Enqueue(EnqueueRequest{ProjectID: projA, Kind: KindMusic, Coalesce: true})
	if err != nil || replayed || music.ID == ids[0] || music.Kind != KindMusic {
		t.Fatalf("other kind = %+v, %v, %v", music, replayed, err)
	}
	other, replayed, err := m.Enqueue(EnqueueRequest{ProjectID: projB, Coalesce: true})
	if err != nil || replayed || other.ID == ids[0] {
		t.Fatalf("other project = %+v, %v, %v", other, replayed, err)
	}
	if _, _, err := m.Enqueue(EnqueueRequest{ProjectID: projA, Kind: KindRender}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a kind outside Kinds was accepted")
	}
}

func TestManagerLoadsOnlyItsKinds(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	now := time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339Nano)
	record := func(id, kind string) string {
		return `{"id":"` + id + `","project_id":"` + projA + `","kind":"` + kind + `","status":"succeeded","progress":1,"frames_done":0,"frames_total":0,"created_at":"` + now + `","updated_at":"` + now + `","rev":1}`
	}
	const renderID, narrateID, musicID = "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa", "bbbbbbbb-bbbb-4bbb-8bbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb", "cccccccc-cccc-4ccc-8ccc-cccccccccccc"
	doc := `{"version":1,"jobs":[` + record(renderID, KindRender) + `,` + record(narrateID, KindNarrate) + `,` + record(musicID, KindMusic) + `]}`
	write := func() {
		t.Helper()
		if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "jobs.json"), []byte(doc), 0o600); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}

	write()
	audio, err := NewManager(ManagerOptions{Dir: dir, Kinds: []string{KindNarrate, KindMusic}, Run: noopJobRun})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, gotRender := audio.Get(renderID)
	_, gotNarrate := audio.Get(narrateID)
	_, gotMusic := audio.Get(musicID)
	audio.Close()
	if gotRender || !gotNarrate || !gotMusic {
		t.Fatalf("narrate manager loaded render %v, narrate %v, music %v", gotRender, gotNarrate, gotMusic)
	}

	write()
	plain := newTestManager(t, dir, noopJobRun)
	_, gotRender = plain.Get(renderID)
	_, gotNarrate = plain.Get(narrateID)
	if !gotRender || gotNarrate {
		t.Fatalf("default manager loaded render %v, narrate %v", gotRender, gotNarrate)
	}
}

func TestManagerRefusesMoreThanOneWorker(t *testing.T) {
	if _, err := NewManager(ManagerOptions{Dir: t.TempDir(), Run: noopJobRun, Workers: 2}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("Workers:2 was accepted")
	}
	for _, workers := range []int{0, 1} {
		m, err := NewManager(ManagerOptions{Dir: t.TempDir(), Run: noopJobRun, Workers: workers})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("Workers:%d = %v", workers, err)
		}
		m.Close()
	}
}

func TestNarrateJobHasNoOutputKeyAndCountsPieces(t *testing.T) {
	entered := make(chan struct{})
	release := make(chan struct{})
	m, err := NewManager(ManagerOptions{Dir: t.TempDir(), Kinds: []string{KindNarrate}, Run: func(ctx context.Context, job Job, _ any, h *Handle) (Output, error) {
		h.SetPhase(PhaseSynthesizing)
		h.SetPieces(1, 4)
		close(entered)
		<-release
		h.SetPieces(4, 4)
		return Output{}, nil
	}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(m.Close)
	job, _, err := m.Enqueue(EnqueueRequest{ProjectID: projA})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	<-entered
	mid, _ := m.Get(job.ID)
	// 1 of 4 pieces is a progress of 0.25.
	if mid.PiecesDone != 1 || mid.PiecesTotal != 4 || mid.Progress != 0.25 || mid.Phase != PhaseSynthesizing {
		t.Fatalf("running job = %+v", mid)
	}
	close(release)
	done := waitJob(t, m, job.ID, terminal)
	raw, err := json.Marshal(done)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	text := string(raw)
	if done.Status != StatusSucceeded || done.Output != nil || strings.Contains(text, `"output"`) {
		t.Fatalf("finished job = %s", text)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(text, `"pieces_done":4`) || !strings.Contains(text, `"pieces_total":4`) {
		t.Fatalf("piece counters are missing: %s", text)
	}
}

func TestJobMessagesSayThisJob(t *testing.T) {
	check := func(name string, j Job) {
		t.Helper()
		if j.Error == nil {
			t.Fatalf("%s: no error on %+v", name, j)
		}
		msg := strings.ToLower(j.Error.Message)
		if !strings.Contains(msg, "this job") || strings.Contains(msg, "export") || strings.Contains(msg, "render") {
			t.Errorf("%s: message = %q", name, j.Error.Message)
		}
	}

	dir := t.TempDir()
	now := time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339Nano)
	const id = "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa"
	doc := `{"version":1,"jobs":[{"id":"` + id + `","project_id":"` + projA + `","kind":"render","status":"running","created_at":"` + now + `","updated_at":"` + now + `","rev":1}]}`
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "jobs.json"), []byte(doc), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	restarted := newTestManager(t, dir, noopJobRun)
	j, _ := restarted.Get(id)
	check("restart", j)

	started := make(chan struct{}, 8)
	m, err := NewManager(ManagerOptions{Dir: t.TempDir(), Run: func(ctx context.Context, job Job, _ any, h *Handle) (Output, error) {
		started <- struct{}{}
		<-ctx.Done()
		return Output{}, ctx.Err()
	}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(m.Close)
	running, _, _ := m.Enqueue(EnqueueRequest{ProjectID: projA})
	<-started
	queued, _, _ := m.Enqueue(EnqueueRequest{ProjectID: projA})
	cancelled, _ := m.Cancel(queued.ID)
	check("cancel of a queued job", cancelled)
	m.Cancel(running.ID)
	check("cancel of a running job", waitJob(t, m, running.ID, terminal))

	running, _, _ = m.Enqueue(EnqueueRequest{ProjectID: projA})
	<-started
	queued, _, _ = m.Enqueue(EnqueueRequest{ProjectID: projA})
	m.Close()
	j, _ = m.Get(running.ID)
	check("close with a running job", j)
	j, _ = m.Get(queued.ID)
	check("close with a queued job", j)
}
```

`Enqueue` head, old:

```go
// Enqueue queues a render. With an Idempotency-Key that was seen for this project and request it
// returns the earlier job and replayed true. ErrBusy when DefaultMaxQueued jobs are waiting.
func (m *Manager) Enqueue(req EnqueueRequest) (job Job, replayed bool, err error) {
	m.mu.Lock()
	defer m.mu.Unlock()
	if m.closed {
		return Job{}, false, ErrClosed
	}
	keyHash := ""
```

new:

```go
// Enqueue queues a job. With an Idempotency-Key that was seen for this project and request it
// returns the earlier job and replayed true, and with Coalesce the queued or running job of this
// project and kind. ErrBusy when MaxQueued jobs are waiting. The coalesce check runs under m.mu in
// the same step as the insert, so two calls cannot create two jobs.
func (m *Manager) Enqueue(req EnqueueRequest) (job Job, replayed bool, err error) {
	m.mu.Lock()
	defer m.mu.Unlock()
	if m.closed {
		return Job{}, false, ErrClosed
	}
	kind := req.Kind
	if kind == "" {
		kind = m.opts.Kinds[0]
	}
	if !m.runsKind(kind) {
		return Job{}, false, fmt.Errorf("storymaker: this job manager does not run %q jobs", kind)
	}
	if req.Coalesce {
		for _, r := range m.jobs {
			if r.ProjectID == req.ProjectID && r.Kind == kind && !r.Terminal() {
				return r.Job, true, nil
			}
		}
	}
	keyHash := ""
```

```go
// SetPieces records the piece counters of a narrate job. It is a state change: it is persisted and
// announced at once, so the throttle cannot swallow the last step.
func (h *Handle) SetPieces(done, total int) {
	h.update(func(r *record) {
		r.PiecesDone, r.PiecesTotal = done, total
		if total > 0 {
			r.Progress = float64(done) / float64(total)
		}
	}, true)
}

// SetPrefixHash stores the hash of the render prefix of this job's listener.
```

```go
// storymakerBlockingRun is a job function that holds its job until it is cancelled.
func storymakerBlockingRun(ctx context.Context, job render.Job, payload any, h *render.Handle) (render.Output, error) {
	<-ctx.Done()
	return render.Output{}, ctx.Err()
}

// storymakerQueueFixture makes a server whose jobs run the given functions. Tests put jobs straight
// on the managers, so no FFmpeg or browser is needed.
func storymakerQueueFixture(t *testing.T, renderRun, audioRun render.RunFunc) (*Server, *storymakerRuntime, string, apiProject) {
	t.Helper()
	s, _, writeToken := storymakerAPIFixture(t)
	s.storymakerRunOverride = renderRun
	s.storymakerAudioRunOverride = audioRun
	p := createStorymakerProject(t, s, writeToken)
	rt, err := s.storymakerRuntime()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return s, rt, writeToken, p
}

func enqueueStorymakerJob(t *testing.T, m *render.Manager, projectID, kind string) render.Job {
	t.Helper()
	job, _, err := m.Enqueue(render.EnqueueRequest{ProjectID: projectID, Kind: kind})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return job
}

func waitStorymakerManagerJob(t *testing.T, m *render.Manager, id string) render.Job {
	t.Helper()
	deadline := time.Now().Add(10 * time.Second)
	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
		if job, ok := m.Get(id); ok && job.Terminal() {
			return job
		}
		time.Sleep(5 * time.Millisecond)
	}
	job, _ := m.Get(id)
	t.Fatalf("job %s did not end: %+v", id, job)
	return render.Job{}
}

// openStorymakerEvents opens the event stream of a project and returns its jobs in the order they arrive.
func openStorymakerEvents(t *testing.T, s *Server, token, projectID string) <-chan render.Job {
	t.Helper()
	srv := httptest.NewServer(handleStorymaker(s))
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30*time.Second)
	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodGet, srv.URL+storymakerAPIBase+"/projects/"+projectID+"/events", nil)
	if err != nil {
		cancel()
		srv.Close()
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+token)
	res, err := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		cancel()
		srv.Close()
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		cancel()
		res.Body.Close()
		srv.Close()
	})
	events := make(chan render.Job, 64)
	go func() {
		defer close(events)
		sc := bufio.NewScanner(res.Body)
		sc.Buffer(make([]byte, 64<<10), 1<<20)
		var name string
		for sc.Scan() {
			line := sc.Text()
			switch {
			case strings.HasPrefix(line, "event: "):
				name = strings.TrimPrefix(line, "event: ")
			case strings.HasPrefix(line, "data: ") && name == "job":
				var d struct {
					Job render.Job `json:"job"`
				}
				if json.Unmarshal([]byte(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "data: ")), &d) == nil {
					events <- d.Job
				}
			}
		}
	}()
	return events
}

func TestStorymakerRouterServesBothQueues(t *testing.T) {
	s, rt, token, p := storymakerQueueFixture(t, storymakerBlockingRun, storymakerBlockingRun)
	// CreatedAt orders the merged list; the pauses keep the three stamps apart on a coarse clock.
	older := enqueueStorymakerJob(t, rt.jobs, p.Project.ID, "")
	time.Sleep(20 * time.Millisecond)
	narrate := enqueueStorymakerJob(t, rt.narrate, p.Project.ID, render.KindNarrate)
	time.Sleep(20 * time.Millisecond)
	newer := enqueueStorymakerJob(t, rt.jobs, p.Project.ID, "")

	w := storymakerRequest(s, token, http.MethodGet, "/jobs/"+narrate.ID, nil, nil)
	var got struct {
		Job render.Job `json:"job"`
	}
	decodeBody(t, w, &got)
	if w.Code != http.StatusOK || got.Job.ID != narrate.ID || got.Job.Kind != render.KindNarrate {
		t.Fatalf("GET narrate job = %d %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
	}

	w = storymakerRequest(s, token, http.MethodGet, "/projects/"+p.Project.ID+"/jobs", nil, nil)
	var listed struct {
		Jobs []render.Job `json:"jobs"`
	}
	decodeBody(t, w, &listed)
	ids := make([]string, 0, len(listed.Jobs))
	for _, job := range listed.Jobs {
		ids = append(ids, job.ID)
	}
	want := []string{newer.ID, narrate.ID, older.ID}
	if strings.Join(ids, ",") != strings.Join(want, ",") {
		t.Fatalf("merged list = %v, want %v", ids, want)
	}

	if w := storymakerRequest(s, token, http.MethodPost, "/jobs/"+narrate.ID+"/cancel", nil, nil); w.Code != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("cancel = %d %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
	}
	if job := waitStorymakerManagerJob(t, rt.narrate, narrate.ID); job.Status != render.StatusCancelled {
		t.Fatalf("cancelled narrate job = %+v", job)
	}
}

func TestStorymakerDeleteProjectCancelsBothQueues(t *testing.T) {
	started := make(chan struct{}, 2)
	// The pause after the cancel stands for a job that still holds a file. The delete has to outlast it.
	run := func(ctx context.Context, job render.Job, payload any, h *render.Handle) (render.Output, error) {
		started <- struct{}{}
		<-ctx.Done()
		time.Sleep(200 * time.Millisecond)
		return render.Output{}, ctx.Err()
	}
	s, rt, token, p := storymakerQueueFixture(t, run, run)
	renderJob := enqueueStorymakerJob(t, rt.jobs, p.Project.ID, "")
	narrateJob := enqueueStorymakerJob(t, rt.narrate, p.Project.ID, render.KindNarrate)
	<-started
	<-started
	if w := storymakerRequest(s, token, http.MethodDelete, "/projects/"+p.Project.ID, nil, nil); w.Code != http.StatusNoContent {
		t.Fatalf("delete = %d %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
	}
	// No polling: both jobs have ended by the time the delete answers.
	if job, _ := rt.jobs.Get(renderJob.ID); job.Status != render.StatusCancelled {
		t.Fatalf("render job when the delete answered = %+v", job)
	}
	if job, _ := rt.narrate.Get(narrateJob.ID); job.Status != render.StatusCancelled {
		t.Fatalf("narrate job when the delete answered = %+v", job)
	}
}

func TestStorymakerEventsDeliverBothQueues(t *testing.T) {

	events := openStorymakerEvents(t, s, token, p.Project.ID)
	renderJob := enqueueStorymakerJob(t, rt.jobs, p.Project.ID, "")
	narrateJob := enqueueStorymakerJob(t, rt.narrate, p.Project.ID, render.KindNarrate)
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	timeout := time.After(10 * time.Second)
	for !seen[renderJob.ID] || !seen[narrateJob.ID] {
		select {
		case job, ok := <-events:
			if !ok {
				t.Fatal("the event stream ended")
			}
			seen[job.ID] = true
		case <-timeout:
			t.Fatalf("events seen: %v", seen)
		}
	}
}

func TestStorymakerEventsFirstListKeepsARunningRender(t *testing.T) {
	started := make(chan struct{}, 1)
	renderRun := func(ctx context.Context, job render.Job, payload any, h *render.Handle) (render.Output, error) {
		started <- struct{}{}
		<-ctx.Done()
		return render.Output{}, ctx.Err()
	}
	audioRun := func(ctx context.Context, job render.Job, payload any, h *render.Handle) (render.Output, error) {
		return render.Output{}, nil
	}
	s, rt, token, p := storymakerQueueFixture(t, renderRun, audioRun)
	running := enqueueStorymakerJob(t, rt.jobs, p.Project.ID, "")
	<-started
	var finished []string
	for i := 0; i < 6; i++ {
		time.Sleep(20 * time.Millisecond)
		job := enqueueStorymakerJob(t, rt.narrate, p.Project.ID, render.KindNarrate)
		waitStorymakerManagerJob(t, rt.narrate, job.ID)
		finished = append(finished, job.ID)
	}
	// Merged newest first: six finished narrate jobs, then the render. The latest five are the newest
	// narrate jobs, and the unfinished rule adds the render. The stream sends oldest first, so the
	// render comes first and the oldest narrate job stays out.
	want := append([]string{running.ID}, finished[1:]...)
	events := openStorymakerEvents(t, s, token, p.Project.ID)
	got := make([]string, 0, len(want))
	timeout := time.After(10 * time.Second)
	for len(got) < len(want) {
		select {
		case job, ok := <-events:
			if !ok {
				t.Fatalf("the event stream ended after %d jobs", len(got))
			}
			got = append(got, job.ID)
		case <-timeout:
			t.Fatalf("first list so far = %v, want %v", got, want)
		}
	}
	if strings.Join(got, ",") != strings.Join(want, ",") {
		t.Fatalf("first list = %v, want %v", got, want)
	}
}
```


new:

```go
// WaitProject blocks until the project has no unfinished job, and returns ctx's error if ctx ends
// first. It cancels nothing. A job turns terminal after its RunFunc has returned, so a nil answer means
// no job of the project still touches its files.
func (m *Manager) WaitProject(ctx context.Context, projectID string) error {
	// Subscribe before the first look, so a job that ends in between still sends its signal.
	changed, unsubscribe := m.Subscribe(projectID)
	defer unsubscribe()
	for {
		if !m.projectBusy(projectID) {
			return nil
		}
		select {
		case <-changed:
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return ctx.Err()
		}
	}
}

// projectBusy reports whether the project has an unfinished job.
func (m *Manager) projectBusy(projectID string) bool {
	m.mu.Lock()
	defer m.mu.Unlock()
	for _, r := range m.jobs {
		if r.ProjectID == projectID && !r.Terminal() {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}
```

```go
// openStorymakerManagers opens the render queue and the narrate queue. A failure of the second closes
// the first, or its worker would stay alive with no owner.
func openStorymakerManagers(dir string, run, audioRun render.RunFunc, logger *slog.Logger, open func(render.ManagerOptions) (*render.Manager, error)) (*render.Manager, *render.Manager, error) {
	jobs, err := open(render.ManagerOptions{Dir: dir, Run: run, Logger: logger})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	narrate, err := open(render.ManagerOptions{
		Dir:       filepath.Join(dir, "narrate"),
		Kinds:     []string{render.KindNarrate, render.KindMusic},
		MaxQueued: storymakerMaxQueuedNarrations,
		Workers:   1,
		Run:       audioRun,
		Logger:    logger,
	})
	if err != nil {
		jobs.Close()
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	return jobs, narrate, nil
}
```

```go
// normalizedStorymakerConfig returns a copy of the configuration with the Storymaker section
```

new:

```go
// jobByID finds a job in either queue and returns the manager that holds it.
func (rt *storymakerRuntime) jobByID(id string) (render.Job, *render.Manager, bool) {
	if job, ok := rt.jobs.Get(id); ok {
		return job, rt.jobs, true
	}
	if job, ok := rt.narrate.Get(id); ok {
		return job, rt.narrate, true
	}
	return render.Job{}, nil, false
}

// listJobs merges the jobs of a project from both queues, newest first.
func (rt *storymakerRuntime) listJobs(projectID string) []render.Job {
	jobs := append(rt.jobs.List(projectID), rt.narrate.List(projectID)...)
	sort.Slice(jobs, func(i, j int) bool {
		if !jobs[i].CreatedAt.Equal(jobs[j].CreatedAt) {
			return jobs[i].CreatedAt.After(jobs[j].CreatedAt)
		}
		return jobs[i].ID > jobs[j].ID
	})
	return jobs
}

// initialJobs is the first list of the event stream, newest first: every unfinished job of both
// queues plus the merged latest five, so newer narrate jobs cannot push a running export out of it.
func (rt *storymakerRuntime) initialJobs(projectID string) []render.Job {
	all := rt.listJobs(projectID)
	out := make([]render.Job, 0, len(all))
	for i, job := range all {
		if i < 5 || !job.Terminal() {
			out = append(out, job)
		}
	}
	return out
}

// cancelProject cancels every unfinished job of a project in both queues.
func (rt *storymakerRuntime) cancelProject(projectID string) {
	rt.jobs.CancelProject(projectID)
	rt.narrate.CancelProject(projectID)
}

// storymakerDeleteWait bounds how long a project delete waits for the project's jobs to stop.
const storymakerDeleteWait = 15 * time.Second

// waitProject waits until the project has no unfinished job in either queue. A job that was cancelled
// can still hold a file open or write one, so a delete waits before it removes the folder. A job that
// outlasts the bound does not block the delete, so the outcome of the wait is not returned.
func (rt *storymakerRuntime) waitProject(ctx context.Context, projectID string) {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, storymakerDeleteWait)
	defer cancel()
	_ = rt.jobs.WaitProject(ctx, projectID)
	_ = rt.narrate.WaitProject(ctx, projectID)
}

// close stops both managers.
func (rt *storymakerRuntime) close() {
	rt.jobs.Close()
	rt.narrate.Close()
}

// normalizedStorymakerConfig returns a copy of the configuration with the Storymaker section
```

```go
package server

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"math"
	"net/http"
	"slices"

	"testing"

	"aurago/internal/config"

	"aurago/internal/storymaker/render"
	"aurago/internal/tools"
)

// Two sentences that stay separate pieces: widths 32 and 36 are at least 20 and their 6 words are
// over 3 (SplitPieces rule 3), and each fits the chunk limit of 2000 of sanotts.
const (
	narrateJobFirst  = "Der erste Satz steht ganz vorne."
	narrateJobSecond = "Der zweite Satz folgt gleich danach."
	narrateJobSano   = `{"provider":"sanotts","voice":"","language":"de"}`
)

func narrateJobStory(voice, narration string) string {
	return `{"schema":"storymaker/1","title":"Stimme","voice":` + voice + `,"scenes":[{"id":"s1","narration":"` + narration + `"}]}`
}

// narrateJobWAV is a valid piece: 1 s of 24 kHz mono s16 with a 440 Hz tone at amplitude 0.1.
func narrateJobWAV() []byte {
	const rate = 24000
	samples := make([]int16, rate)
	for i := range samples {
		samples[i] = int16(3276 * math.Sin(2*math.Pi*440*float64(i)/rate))
	}
	var b bytes.Buffer
	b.WriteString("RIFF")
	_ = binary.Write(&b, binary.LittleEndian, uint32(36+2*len(samples)))
	b.WriteString("WAVEfmt ")
	for _, v := range []any{uint32(16), uint16(1), uint16(1), uint32(rate), uint32(2 * rate), uint16(2), uint16(16)} {
		_ = binary.Write(&b, binary.LittleEndian, v)
	}
	b.WriteString("data")
	_ = binary.Write(&b, binary.LittleEndian, uint32(2*len(samples)))
	_ = binary.Write(&b, binary.LittleEndian, samples)
	return b.Bytes()
}

// narrateJobRecorder is a synth stub that keeps the texts it was asked for and answers a valid piece.
func narrateJobRecorder(calls *[]string) func(context.Context, tools.TTSConfig, string) ([]byte, string, error) {
	return func(_ context.Context, _ tools.TTSConfig, text string) ([]byte, string, error) {
		*calls = append(*calls, text)
		return narrateJobWAV(), "wav", nil
	}
}

func narrateJobSetConfig(s *Server, edit func(*config.Config)) {
	s.CfgMu.Lock()
	defer s.CfgMu.Unlock()
	next := *s.ConfigSnapshot()
	edit(&next)
	s.replaceConfigSnapshot(&next)
}

type narrateJobEnv struct {
	s     *Server
	rt    *storymakerRuntime
	token string
	id    string
	etag  string
}

func newNarrateJobEnv(t *testing.T) *narrateJobEnv {
	t.Helper()
	s, _, token := storymakerAPIFixture(t)
	// Plenty of room, so a nearly full test disk cannot change a result.
	s.storymakerFreeDiskOverride = func(string) (int64, error) { return 1 << 40, nil }
	p := createStorymakerProject(t, s, token)
	rt, err := s.storymakerRuntime()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return &narrateJobEnv{s: s, rt: rt, token: token, id: p.Project.ID, etag: p.ETag}
}

// save stores a story. It returns an error instead of failing, because stubs call it on the job goroutine.
func (e *narrateJobEnv) save(story string) error {
	w := storymakerRequest(e.s, e.token, http.MethodPut, "/projects/"+e.id+"/story", strings.NewReader(story), map[string]string{"If-Match": e.etag})
	if w.Code != http.StatusOK {
		return fmt.Errorf("save = %d %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
	}
	var saved struct {
		ETag string `json:"etag"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(w.Body.Bytes(), &saved); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	e.etag = saved.ETag
	return nil
}

func (e *narrateJobEnv) mustSave(t *testing.T, story string) {
	t.Helper()
	if err := e.save(story); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// run enqueues a narrate job and waits for its end.
func (e *narrateJobEnv) run(t *testing.T) render.Job {
	t.Helper()
	job, _, err := e.rt.narrate.Enqueue(render.EnqueueRequest{ProjectID: e.id, Kind: render.KindNarrate, Coalesce: true, Payload: storymakerNarratePayload{ProjectID: e.id}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	deadline := time.Now().Add(20 * time.Second)
	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
		if done, ok := e.rt.narrate.Get(job.ID); ok && done.Terminal() {
			return done
		}
		time.Sleep(5 * time.Millisecond)
	}
	last, _ := e.rt.narrate.Get(job.ID)
	t.Fatalf("the narrate job did not end: %+v", last)
	return render.Job{}
}

func (e *narrateJobEnv) missing(t *testing.T) []storymaker.MissingBlock {
	t.Helper()
	ctx := context.Background()
	data, _, err := e.rt.projects.LoadStory(ctx, e.id)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	story, _, err := storymaker.DecodeStory(data, storymaker.DefaultLimits())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	storymaker.ApplyDefaults(&story)
	answer, err := e.rt.projects.AudioAnswer(ctx, e.id, &story, nil)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return answer.Missing
}

func requireNarrateFailure(t *testing.T, job render.Job, code string) {
	t.Helper()
	if job.Status != render.StatusFailed || job.Error == nil || job.Error.Code != code {
		t.Fatalf("job = %+v, want failed with %s", job, code)
	}
}

func TestStorymakerTTSConfigFollowsTheVoice(t *testing.T) {
	cfg := &config.Config{}
	cfg.TTS.Provider = "google"
	cfg.TTS.Language = "en"
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		provider string
		get      func(tools.TTSConfig) string
	}{
		{"phonon", func(c tools.TTSConfig) string { return c.Phonon.VoiceID }},
		{"piper", func(c tools.TTSConfig) string { return c.Piper.Voice }},
		{"supertonic", func(c tools.TTSConfig) string { return c.Supertonic.Voice }},
		{"xai", func(c tools.TTSConfig) string { return c.XAI.VoiceID }},
		{"openai", func(c tools.TTSConfig) string { return c.OpenAI.VoiceID }},
		{"stepfun", func(c tools.TTSConfig) string { return c.StepFun.VoiceID }},
		{"elevenlabs", func(c tools.TTSConfig) string { return c.ElevenLabs.VoiceID }},
		{"minimax", func(c tools.TTSConfig) string { return c.MiniMax.VoiceID }},
		{"mistral", func(c tools.TTSConfig) string { return c.Mistral.VoiceID }},
		{"resemble", func(c tools.TTSConfig) string { return c.Resemble.VoiceID }},
	} {
		got := storymakerTTSConfig(cfg, storymaker.Voice{Provider: tc.provider, Voice: "voice-1", Language: "de"})
		if got.Provider != tc.provider || got.Language != "de" || !got.NoProviderFallback || tc.get(got) != "voice-1" {
			t.Errorf("%s: %+v", tc.provider, got)
		}
	}
	if got := storymakerTTSConfig(cfg, storymaker.Voice{Provider: "supertonic", Language: "de"}); got.Supertonic.ResponseFormat != "wav" {
		t.Errorf("supertonic format = %q", got.Supertonic.ResponseFormat)
	}
	// BuildTTSConfig answers cfg.TTS.Language ("en") for sanotts (tts_config.go:62-68), so "de" proves the second set.
	if got := storymakerTTSConfig(cfg, storymaker.Voice{Provider: "sanotts", Language: "de"}); got.Language != "de" {
		t.Errorf("sanotts language = %q", got.Language)
	}
	if cfg.TTS.Provider != "google" {
		t.Errorf("the caller's config changed: %q", cfg.TTS.Provider)
	}
}

func TestStorymakerNarrateSkipsASentenceDeletedDuringTheRun(t *testing.T) {
	env := newNarrateJobEnv(t)
	env.mustSave(t, narrateJobStory(narrateJobSano, narrateJobFirst+" "+narrateJobSecond))
	var calls []string
	env.s.storymakerSynthOverride = func(_ context.Context, _ tools.TTSConfig, text string) ([]byte, string, error) {
		calls = append(calls, text)
		if err := env.save(narrateJobStory(narrateJobSano, narrateJobFirst)); err != nil {
			t.Error(err)
		}
		return narrateJobWAV(), "wav", nil
	}
	job := env.run(t)
	if job.Status != render.StatusSucceeded || !slices.Equal(calls, []string{narrateJobFirst}) {
		t.Fatalf("job = %+v, calls = %q", job, calls)
	}
}

func TestStorymakerNarrateReadOnlyStopsABlockedProviderCall(t *testing.T) {
	env := newNarrateJobEnv(t)
	env.mustSave(t, narrateJobStory(narrateJobSano, narrateJobFirst))
	entered := make(chan struct{})
	env.s.storymakerSynthOverride = func(ctx context.Context, _ tools.TTSConfig, _ string) ([]byte, string, error) {
		close(entered)
		<-ctx.Done()
		return nil, "", ctx.Err()
	}
	go func() {
		<-entered
		narrateJobSetConfig(env.s, func(c *config.Config) { c.VirtualDesktop.ReadOnly = true })
	}()
	job := env.run(t)
	requireNarrateFailure(t, job, render.CodeNarrationUnavailable)
	if want := "The desktop became read-only or its configuration changed, so the narration stopped."; job.Error.Message != want {
		t.Errorf("message = %q, want %q", job.Error.Message, want)
	}
	files, err := env.rt.projects.PieceFiles(context.Background(), env.id)
	if err != nil || len(files) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("stored files = %v, %v", files, err)
	}
}

func TestStorymakerNarrateStopsWhenStorymakerIsSwitchedOff(t *testing.T) {

	}
}

func TestStorymakerNarrateUndecodableAudioNamesTheProvider(t *testing.T) {
	env := newNarrateJobEnv(t)
	env.mustSave(t, narrateJobStory(narrateJobSano, narrateJobFirst))

	}

func TestStorymakerNarrateRefusesAnOversizedProviderAnswer(t *testing.T) {
	env := newNarrateJobEnv(t)
	env.mustSave(t, narrateJobStory(narrateJobSano, narrateJobFirst))
	oversized := make([]byte, storymakerMaxPieceBytes+1)
	env.s.storymakerSynthOverride = func(context.Context, tools.TTSConfig, string) ([]byte, string, error) {
		return oversized, "wav", nil
	}
	job := env.run(t)
	requireNarrateFailure(t, job, render.CodeNarrationFailed)
	// These zero bytes would fail DecodeAudio with "could not be decoded", so this text shows it never ran.
	if !strings.Contains(job.Error.Message, "256 MiB") {
		t.Errorf("message = %q", job.Error.Message)
	}
	if files, err := env.rt.projects.PieceFiles(context.Background(), env.id); err != nil || len(files) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("stored files = %v, %v", files, err)
	}
	// The refusal keeps the bytes: the provider answered, so it has billed.
	data, err := env.s.storymakerSynth(context.Background(), env.s.ConfigSnapshot(), storymaker.Voice{Provider: "sanotts", Language: "de"}, narrateJobFirst)
	if !errors.Is(err, errStorymakerPieceTooLarge) || len(data) == 0 {
		t.Fatalf("direct call = %d bytes, %v", len(data), err)
	}
}

func TestStorymakerNarrateLowDiskNamesTheFreeSize(t *testing.T) {
	env := newNarrateJobEnv(t)
	env.mustSave(t, narrateJobStory(narrateJobSano, narrateJobFirst))
	workspace := env.s.ConfigSnapshot().VirtualDesktop.WorkspaceDir
	var probed string
	env.s.storymakerFreeDiskOverride = func(path string) (int64, error) {
		probed = path
		return 12 << 20, nil
	}
	var calls []string
	env.s.storymakerSynthOverride = narrateJobRecorder(&calls)
	job := env.run(t)
	requireNarrateFailure(t, job, render.CodeNarrationFailed)
	if !strings.Contains(job.Error.Message, "12 MiB") || len(calls) != 0 || probed != workspace {
		t.Fatalf("message = %q, calls = %q, probed = %q", job.Error.Message, calls, probed)
	}
}

func TestStorymakerNarrateReplacesAStoredPieceThatNoLongerDecodes(t *testing.T) {
	env := newNarrateJobEnv(t)
	env.mustSave(t, narrateJobStory(narrateJobSano, narrateJobFirst))
	ctx := context.Background()
	hash := storymaker.PieceKey(storymaker.Voice{Provider: "sanotts", Language: "de"}, narrateJobFirst)
	path, err := env.rt.projects.AudioPath(env.id, "narration", hash+".wav")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := env.rt.store.WriteBytes(ctx, path, []byte("damaged")); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var calls []string
	env.s.storymakerSynthOverride = narrateJobRecorder(&calls)
	job := env.run(t)
	if job.Status != render.StatusSucceeded || !slices.Equal(calls, []string{narrateJobFirst}) {
		t.Fatalf("job = %+v, calls = %q", job, calls)
	}
	rc, _, err := env.rt.projects.OpenAudio(ctx, env.id, "narration", hash+".wav")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer rc.Close()
	data, err := io.ReadAll(rc)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, _, err := storymaker.DecodeAudio(data); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the stored piece still does not decode: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestStorymakerNarrateRerunSkipsStoredPieces(t *testing.T) {
	env := newNarrateJobEnv(t)
	env.mustSave(t, narrateJobStory(narrateJobSano, narrateJobFirst+" "+narrateJobSecond))
	env.s.storymakerSynthOverride = func(_ context.Context, _ tools.TTSConfig, text string) ([]byte, string, error) {
		if text == narrateJobSecond {
			return nil, "", errors.New("provider down")
		}
		return narrateJobWAV(), "wav", nil
	}
	first := env.run(t)
	requireNarrateFailure(t, first, render.CodeNarrationFailed)
	if !strings.Contains(first.Error.Message, "sanotts") {
		t.Errorf("message = %q", first.Error.Message)
	}

	// Without its envelope the stored piece counts as missing, and the rerun rebuilds it with no provider call.
	ctx := context.Background()
	hash := storymaker.PieceKey(storymaker.Voice{Provider: "sanotts", Language: "de"}, narrateJobFirst)
	audioPath, err := env.rt.projects.AudioPath(env.id, "narration", hash+".wav")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := env.rt.store.Remove(ctx, strings.TrimSuffix(audioPath, ".wav")+".env.json"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	var calls []string
	env.s.storymakerSynthOverride = narrateJobRecorder(&calls)
	second := env.run(t)
	if second.Status != render.StatusSucceeded || !slices.Equal(calls, []string{narrateJobSecond}) {
		t.Fatalf("rerun = %+v, calls = %q", second, calls)
	}
	// Two pieces, both stored at the end.
	if second.PiecesDone != 2 || second.PiecesTotal != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("pieces = %d of %d", second.PiecesDone, second.PiecesTotal)
	}
	if missing := env.missing(t); len(missing) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("still missing: %+v", missing)
	}
}
```

Replace the whole of `internal/server/storymaker_narrate.go` as N10 left it with:

```go
package server

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"io"
	"log/slog"

	"aurago/internal/config"
	"aurago/internal/fileutil"
	"aurago/internal/security"
	"aurago/internal/storymaker"
	"aurago/internal/storymaker/render"
	"aurago/internal/tools"
)

// storymakerMaxPieceBytes bounds one piece: a stored file read back, because workspace files are not
// trusted, and a provider's answer, before it is decoded.
const storymakerMaxPieceBytes = 256 << 20

// errStorymakerPieceTooLarge refuses a provider answer over storymakerMaxPieceBytes.
var errStorymakerPieceTooLarge = errors.New("it returned more than 256 MiB of audio for one piece")

// storymakerNarratePayload is the payload of a narrate job.
type storymakerNarratePayload struct{ ProjectID string }

// storymakerNarrateRun is the state of one narrate job.
type storymakerNarrateRun struct {
	rt *storymakerRuntime
	id string
	h  *render.Handle
	// verified holds the hashes whose stored file decoded during this job, so each file is decoded once.
	verified map[string]bool
}

// storymakerNarrateTarget is the next piece that needs work.
type storymakerNarrateTarget struct {
	block storymaker.NarrationBlock
	piece storymaker.Piece
	hash  string
	ext   string // the format of the stored file; "" when no audio exists
}

// storymakerTTSConfig builds the speech settings of one voice. It works on a copy of cfg.
func storymakerTTSConfig(cfg *config.Config, v storymaker.Voice) tools.TTSConfig {
	cp := *cfg
	cp.TTS.Provider = v.Provider
	tc := tools.BuildTTSConfig(&cp, tools.TTSRouteTool, v.Language)
	// BuildTTSConfig replaces the language for phonon and sanotts (tts_config.go:62-68).
	tc.Language = v.Language
	tc.NoProviderFallback = true
	switch storymaker.NormalizeProvider(v.Provider) {
	case "phonon":
		tc.Phonon.VoiceID = v.Voice
	case "piper":
		tc.Piper.Voice = v.Voice
	case "supertonic":
		tc.Supertonic.Voice = v.Voice
		tc.Supertonic.ResponseFormat = "wav"
	case "xai":
		tc.XAI.VoiceID = v.Voice
	case "openai":
		tc.OpenAI.VoiceID = v.Voice
	case "stepfun":
		tc.StepFun.VoiceID = v.Voice
	case "elevenlabs":
		tc.ElevenLabs.VoiceID = v.Voice
	case "minimax":
		tc.MiniMax.VoiceID = v.Voice
	case "mistral":
		tc.Mistral.VoiceID = v.Voice
	case "resemble":
		tc.Resemble.VoiceID = v.Voice
	}
	return tc
}

// storymakerSynth makes one provider call. An answer over storymakerMaxPieceBytes is refused here,
// before DecodeAudio can spend memory on its samples. The refusal still returns the bytes: a provider
// that answered has billed, and the caller can tell that from them.
func (s *Server) storymakerSynth(ctx context.Context, cfg *config.Config, v storymaker.Voice, text string) ([]byte, error) {
	synth := tools.TTSSynthesizeInMemoryContext
	if s.storymakerSynthOverride != nil {
		synth = s.storymakerSynthOverride
	}
	data, _, err := synth(ctx, storymakerTTSConfig(cfg, v), text)
	if err == nil && len(data) > storymakerMaxPieceBytes {
		err = errStorymakerPieceTooLarge
	}
	return data, err
}

func (s *Server) storymakerFreeDisk(path string) (int64, error) {
	if s.storymakerFreeDiskOverride != nil {
		return s.storymakerFreeDiskOverride(path)
	}
	return fileutil.FreeDiskBytes(path)
}

// storymakerAudioRun runs a job of the narrate queue. It gets rt directly, because
// s.storymakerRuntime() holds storymakerMu while a replaced runtime waits for its running job. The
// desktop run makes a config change that sets read-only or switches the desktop off cancel a provider
// call already in flight. Every error it returns is a *render.JobError, because a plain
// context.Canceled would surface as render_failed.
func (s *Server) storymakerAudioRun(ctx context.Context, rt *storymakerRuntime, job render.Job, payload any, h *render.Handle) (render.Output, error) {
	ctx, release, err := s.beginDesktopRun(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return render.Output{}, s.storymakerNarrateStopped()
	}
	defer release()
	if job.Kind != render.KindNarrate {
		return render.Output{}, render.Fail(render.CodeNarrationFailed, "Jobs of kind %q are not available.", job.Kind)
	}
	run := &storymakerNarrateRun{rt: rt, id: job.ProjectID, h: h, verified: map[string]bool{}}
	h.SetPhase(render.PhaseSynthesizing)
	for {
		if ctx.Err() != nil {
			return render.Output{}, s.storymakerNarrateStopped()
		}
		more, err := s.storymakerNarrateStep(ctx, run)
		if err != nil {
			return render.Output{}, err
		}
		if !more {
			return render.Output{}, nil
		}
	}
}

// storymakerNarrateStep does one round: it re-reads everything and then makes at most one provider
// call. It reports whether another round is needed.
func (s *Server) storymakerNarrateStep(ctx context.Context, run *storymakerNarrateRun) (bool, error) {
	// Re-read the story and the piece list: an edit during the run counts, and deleted text is not synthesised.
	story, answer, files, readErr := s.storymakerNarrateRead(ctx, run)
	// The configuration of this moment. It goes ahead of a read error, because a desktop that was
	// switched off also fails its reads.
	cfg, err := s.storymakerNarrateGate()
	if err != nil {
		return false, err
	}
	if readErr != nil {
		return false, s.storymakerNarrateFailed(ctx, readErr)
	}

	target, noVoice, done, total := run.scan(&story, answer, files)
	run.h.SetPieces(done, total)
	if len(answer.Missing) == 0 {
		return false, nil
	}
	// A save during the run can set the voice to null, and BuildTTSConfig would turn an empty provider
	// into piper (tts_config.go:38-41).
	if noVoice != "" {
		return false, render.Fail(render.CodeVoiceRequired, "Block %s has no voice. Choose a voice and generate the narration again.", noVoice)
	}
	if target == nil {
		return false, render.Fail(render.CodeNarrationFailed, "Every piece is stored, but some blocks still have no usable envelope.")
	}
	// A stored file needs no provider call: it either decodes and gets its envelope, or it is removed.
	if target.ext != "" {
		return true, s.storymakerVerifyPiece(ctx, run, target)
	}

	// Too little disk fails before anything is paid for.
	if free, err := s.storymakerFreeDisk(cfg.VirtualDesktop.WorkspaceDir); err == nil && free < render.MinFreeDiskBytes {
		return false, render.Fail(render.CodeNarrationFailed, "Only %d MiB are free in the desktop workspace; narration needs at least %d MiB.", free>>20, render.MinFreeDiskBytes>>20)
	}
	// One provider call. The stored format is the detected one, never the provider's claim.
	provider := target.block.Voice.Provider
	data, err := s.storymakerSynth(ctx, cfg, target.block.Voice, target.piece.Text)
	if err != nil {
		if ctx.Err() != nil {
			return false, s.storymakerNarrateStopped()
		}
		return false, render.Fail(render.CodeNarrationFailed, "The voice provider %s failed on block %s: %s", provider, target.block.ID, storymakerProviderMessage(err))
	}
	ext, env, err := storymakerDecodePiece(data)
	if err != nil {
		return false, render.Fail(render.CodeNarrationFailed, "The voice provider %s returned audio that could not be decoded.", provider)
	}
	if err := run.rt.projects.WritePiece(ctx, run.id, target.hash, ext, data, env); err != nil {
		return false, s.storymakerNarrateFailed(ctx, err)
	}
	run.verified[target.hash] = true
	run.h.SetPieces(done+1, total)
	return true, nil
}

// storymakerNarrateRead reads the story, the audio answer and the piece list of this moment.
func (s *Server) storymakerNarrateRead(ctx context.Context, run *storymakerNarrateRun) (storymaker.Story, storymaker.AudioAnswer, map[string]storymaker.PieceFile, error) {
	// The desktop service can be replaced while a job runs. Requests refresh the store the same way.
	if svc, _, err := s.getDesktopService(ctx); err == nil {
		run.rt.store.use(svc)
	}
	data, _, err := run.rt.projects.LoadStory(ctx, run.id)
	if err != nil {
		return storymaker.Story{}, storymaker.AudioAnswer{}, nil, err
	}
	story, _, err := storymaker.DecodeStory(data, storymaker.DefaultLimits())
	if err != nil {
		return storymaker.Story{}, storymaker.AudioAnswer{}, nil, render.Fail(render.CodeInvalidStory, "The story can no longer be read, so the narration stopped.")
	}
	storymaker.ApplyDefaults(&story)
	answer, err := run.rt.projects.AudioAnswer(ctx, run.id, &story, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return storymaker.Story{}, storymaker.AudioAnswer{}, nil, err
	}
	files, err := run.rt.projects.PieceFiles(ctx, run.id)
	if err != nil {
		return storymaker.Story{}, storymaker.AudioAnswer{}, nil, err
	}
	return story, answer, files, nil
}

// storymakerNarrateGate checks the configuration of this moment.
func (s *Server) storymakerNarrateGate() (*config.Config, error) {
	cfg, err := normalizedStorymakerConfig(s)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, render.Fail(render.CodeNarrationUnavailable, "The Storymaker configuration is invalid: %s", err)
	}
	if !cfg.Storymaker.Enabled || !cfg.VirtualDesktop.Enabled {
		return nil, render.Fail(render.CodeDisabled, "Storymaker was switched off during this narration.")
	}
	if cfg.VirtualDesktop.ReadOnly {
		return nil, render.Fail(render.CodeNarrationUnavailable, "The desktop is read-only, so the narration stopped.")
	}
	return cfg, nil
}

// scan finds the first piece that needs work. A block outside answer.Missing has all its pieces.
// Inside a missing block a stored file counts once it decoded during this job.
func (run *storymakerNarrateRun) scan(story *storymaker.Story, answer storymaker.AudioAnswer, files map[string]storymaker.PieceFile) (target *storymakerNarrateTarget, noVoice string, done, total int) {
	missing := make(map[string]bool, len(answer.Missing))
	for _, m := range answer.Missing {
		missing[m.BlockID] = true
	}
	for _, b := range storymaker.Blocks(story) {
		blockMissing := missing[b.ID]
		if blockMissing && b.Voice.Provider == "" && noVoice == "" {
			noVoice = b.ID
		}
		for _, piece := range storymaker.PiecesOf(b) {
			total++
			hash := storymaker.PieceKey(b.Voice, piece.Text)
			ext := files[hash].Ext
			if !blockMissing || (ext != "" && run.verified[hash]) {
				done++
				continue
			}
			if target == nil {
				target = &storymakerNarrateTarget{block: b, piece: piece, hash: hash, ext: ext}
			}
		}
	}
	return target, noVoice, done, total
}

// storymakerVerifyPiece checks a stored file with no provider call. A file that decodes gets a new
// envelope. A file that does not decode is removed, so a damaged file cannot fail a project forever,
// and the next round synthesises it again.
func (s *Server) storymakerVerifyPiece(ctx context.Context, run *storymakerNarrateRun, target *storymakerNarrateTarget) error {
	data, err := storymakerReadPiece(ctx, run, target)
	if err != nil {
		return s.storymakerNarrateFailed(ctx, err)
	}
	ext, env, err := storymakerDecodePiece(data)
	if err != nil || ext != target.ext {
		if err := run.rt.projects.RemovePiece(ctx, run.id, target.hash, target.ext); err != nil {
			return s.storymakerNarrateFailed(ctx, err)
		}
		return nil
	}
	if err := run.rt.projects.WriteEnvelope(ctx, run.id, target.hash, env); err != nil {
		return s.storymakerNarrateFailed(ctx, err)
	}
	run.verified[target.hash] = true
	return nil
}

// storymakerReadPiece reads a stored file. A file over the bound comes back as nil data, which does not decode.
func storymakerReadPiece(ctx context.Context, run *storymakerNarrateRun, target *storymakerNarrateTarget) ([]byte, error) {
	rc, _, err := run.rt.projects.OpenAudio(ctx, run.id, "narration", target.hash+"."+target.ext)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer rc.Close()
	data, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(rc, storymakerMaxPieceBytes+1))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if len(data) > storymakerMaxPieceBytes {
		return nil, nil
	}
	return data, nil
}

// storymakerDecodePiece decodes audio from its bytes and computes its envelope. Empty audio counts as
// undecodable, because ParseEnvelope would refuse its envelope on every later read.
func storymakerDecodePiece(data []byte) (string, storymaker.Envelope, error) {
	decoded, ext, err := storymaker.DecodeAudio(data)
	if err != nil {
		return "", storymaker.Envelope{}, err
	}
	env := storymaker.ComputeEnvelope(decoded)
	if env.DurationMS <= 0 {
		return "", storymaker.Envelope{}, errors.New("storymaker: the audio is empty")
	}
	return ext, env, nil
}

// storymakerNarrateStopped is the error of a run that lost its context without a cancel request. The
// job manager turns a requested cancel or a shutdown into its own end state, whatever this returns.
// It names both causes, because a revoked run can see its cancelled ctx before the new configuration
// is published (server.go revokes first and stores the snapshot later).
func (s *Server) storymakerNarrateStopped() error {
	return render.Fail(render.CodeNarrationUnavailable, "The desktop became read-only or its configuration changed, so the narration stopped.")
}

// storymakerNarrateFailed maps a storage or read error to the error of the job.
func (s *Server) storymakerNarrateFailed(ctx context.Context, err error) error {
	if ctx.Err() != nil {
		return s.storymakerNarrateStopped()
	}
	var jobErr *render.JobError
	if errors.As(err, &jobErr) {
		return jobErr
	}
	logger := s.Logger
	if logger == nil {
		logger = slog.Default()
	}
	logger.Warn("[Storymaker] A narrate job could not use the project storage", "error", err)
	return render.Fail(render.CodeNarrationFailed, "The narration could not use the project storage.")
}

// storymakerProviderMessage makes a provider error fit for the job document: secrets scrubbed, 200 runes at most.
func storymakerProviderMessage(err error) string {
	text := []rune(security.Scrub(err.Error()))
	if len(text) > 200 {
		return string(text[:200]) + "..."
	}
	return string(text)
}
```

```go
const narrateJobOpenAI = `{"provider":"openai","voice":"alloy","language":"de"}`

// A fixed noon, so a test run across midnight cannot change the date.
var narrateUsageDay = time.Date(2026, 11, 4, 12, 0, 0, 0, time.Local)

func newNarrateUsageEnv(t *testing.T) *narrateJobEnv {
	t.Helper()
	env := newNarrateJobEnv(t)
	env.rt.usage.now = func() time.Time { return narrateUsageDay }
	return env
}

// narrateUsageChars reads the count on disk, -1 when the file is unusable. Stubs call it on the job goroutine.
func narrateUsageChars(path string) int {
	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil {
		return -1
	}
	var cur storymakerUsageFile
	if json.Unmarshal(data, &cur) != nil {
		return -1
	}
	return cur.Chars
}

func TestStorymakerNarrateCloudCapsRefuseBeforeTheCall(t *testing.T) {
	// 135 code points (26 x 5 + 5) in one sentence: over the job cap of 100, under the chunk limit 400 of openai.
	long := strings.Repeat("wort ", 26) + "ende."
	t.Run("job cap", func(t *testing.T) {
		env := newNarrateUsageEnv(t)
		narrateJobSetConfig(env.s, func(c *config.Config) { c.Storymaker.Narration.MaxJobChars = 100 })
		env.mustSave(t, narrateJobStory(narrateJobOpenAI, long))
		var calls []string
		env.s.storymakerSynthOverride = narrateJobRecorder(&calls)
		requireNarrateFailure(t, env.run(t), render.CodeBudgetExceeded)
		if len(calls) != 0 {
			t.Fatalf("provider calls = %q", calls)
		}
		if _, err := os.Stat(env.rt.usage.path); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
			t.Fatalf("a refused piece touched usage.json: %v", err)
		}
	})
	t.Run("daily cap", func(t *testing.T) {
		env := newNarrateUsageEnv(t)
		narrateJobSetConfig(env.s, func(c *config.Config) { c.Storymaker.Narration.MaxDailyChars = 1000 })
		env.mustSave(t, narrateJobStory(narrateJobOpenAI, narrateJobFirst))
		// 995 counted: 995 + 32 code points of the sentence is over the cap of 1000.
		if err := os.WriteFile(env.rt.usage.path, []byte(`{"v":1,"date":"2026-11-04","chars":995}`), 0o600); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		var calls []string
		env.s.storymakerSynthOverride = narrateJobRecorder(&calls)
		requireNarrateFailure(t, env.run(t), render.CodeBudgetExceeded)
		if len(calls) != 0 || narrateUsageChars(env.rt.usage.path) != 995 {
			t.Fatalf("calls = %q, chars = %d", calls, narrateUsageChars(env.rt.usage.path))
		}
	})
}

func TestStorymakerNarrateLocalVoiceIsExemptInAnyCase(t *testing.T) {
	env := newNarrateUsageEnv(t)
	narrateJobSetConfig(env.s, func(c *config.Config) {
		c.Storymaker.Narration.MaxJobChars = 100
		c.Storymaker.Narration.MaxDailyChars = 1000
	})

	long := strings.Repeat("wort ", 26) + "ende."

	var calls []string
	env.s.storymakerSynthOverride = narrateJobRecorder(&calls)

	if job.Status != render.StatusSucceeded || !slices.Equal(calls, []string{long}) {
		t.Fatalf("job = %+v, calls = %q", job, calls)
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(env.rt.usage.path); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
		t.Fatalf("a local voice touched usage.json: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestStorymakerNarrateUsageIsWrittenBeforeTheCallAndKeptOnceAnswered(t *testing.T) {
	env := newNarrateUsageEnv(t)
	env.mustSave(t, narrateJobStory(narrateJobOpenAI, narrateJobFirst))
	path := env.rt.usage.path
	// The one piece has 32 code points.
	const charge = 32

	during := -1
	env.s.storymakerSynthOverride = func(context.Context, tools.TTSConfig, string) ([]byte, string, error) {
		during = narrateUsageChars(path)
		return nil, "", errors.New("provider down")
	}
	requireNarrateFailure(t, env.run(t), render.CodeNarrationFailed)
	if during != charge || narrateUsageChars(path) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("during the call %d, after it %d, want %d and 0", during, narrateUsageChars(path), charge)
	}

	// The provider answered, so it has billed: bytes that do not decode keep the reservation.
	env.s.storymakerSynthOverride = func(context.Context, tools.TTSConfig, string) ([]byte, string, error) {
		return []byte("this is not audio"), "mp3", nil
	}
	requireNarrateFailure(t, env.run(t), render.CodeNarrationFailed)
	if got := narrateUsageChars(path); got != charge {
		t.Fatalf("after undecodable audio %d, want %d", got, charge)
	}

	// An error that still delivered bytes keeps it too.
	env.s.storymakerSynthOverride = func(context.Context, tools.TTSConfig, string) ([]byte, string, error) {
		return []byte("partial"), "mp3", errors.New("cut off")
	}
	requireNarrateFailure(t, env.run(t), render.CodeNarrationFailed)
	if got := narrateUsageChars(path); got != 2*charge {
		t.Fatalf("after an error with bytes %d, want %d", got, 2*charge)
	}
}

func TestStorymakerNarrateJobCapCountsCharactersAlreadySent(t *testing.T) {
	env := newNarrateUsageEnv(t)
	narrateJobSetConfig(env.s, func(c *config.Config) { c.Storymaker.Narration.MaxJobChars = 100 })
	// Two sentences of 65 code points (12 x 5 + 5): each fits the cap of 100, together they do not.
	one := strings.Repeat("wort ", 12) + "ende."
	two := strings.Repeat("Satz ", 12) + "ende."
	env.mustSave(t, narrateJobStory(narrateJobOpenAI, one+" "+two))
	var calls []string
	env.s.storymakerSynthOverride = narrateJobRecorder(&calls)
	requireNarrateFailure(t, env.run(t), render.CodeBudgetExceeded)
	if !slices.Equal(calls, []string{one}) || narrateUsageChars(env.rt.usage.path) != 65 {
		t.Fatalf("calls = %q, chars = %d", calls, narrateUsageChars(env.rt.usage.path))
	}
}

func TestStorymakerNarrateUnreadableUsageStopsCloudCalls(t *testing.T) {
	env := newNarrateUsageEnv(t)
	narrateJobSetConfig(env.s, func(c *config.Config) { c.Storymaker.Narration.MaxDailyChars = 1000 })
	env.mustSave(t, narrateJobStory(narrateJobOpenAI, narrateJobFirst))
	path := env.rt.usage.path
	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte("not json"), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var calls []string
	env.s.storymakerSynthOverride = narrateJobRecorder(&calls)
	job := env.run(t)
	requireNarrateFailure(t, job, render.CodeBudgetExceeded)
	if !strings.Contains(job.Error.Message, "usage.bad.json") || len(calls) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("message = %q, calls = %q", job.Error.Message, calls)
	}
	bad, err := os.ReadFile(strings.TrimSuffix(path, "usage.json") + "usage.bad.json")
	if err != nil || string(bad) != "not json" {
		t.Fatalf("usage.bad.json = %q, %v", bad, err)
	}
	// The replacement holds the whole cap, so the guard does not fail open.
	if got := narrateUsageChars(path); got != 1000 {
		t.Fatalf("usage.json holds %d, want 1000", got)
	}
}

func TestStorymakerUsageResetsOnANewLocalDate(t *testing.T) {
	u := newStorymakerNarrationUsage(t.TempDir())
	day := time.Date(2026, 11, 4, 23, 59, 0, 0, time.Local)
	u.now = func() time.Time { return day }
	if err := u.reserve(900, 1000); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// 900 + 200 is over the cap of 1000.
	var refused *render.JobError
	if err := u.reserve(200, 1000); !errors.As(err, &refused) || refused.Code != render.CodeBudgetExceeded {
		t.Fatalf("second reserve = %v", err)
	}
	day = day.Add(2 * time.Minute) // 00:01 on the next local date
	if err := u.reserve(200, 1000); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("reserve on the new date = %v", err)
	}
	used, err := u.today(1000)
	if err != nil || used != 200 {
		t.Fatalf("today = %d, %v", used, err)
	}
	data, err := os.ReadFile(u.path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var cur storymakerUsageFile
	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &cur); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if cur.V != 1 || cur.Date != "2026-11-05" || cur.Chars != 200 {
		t.Fatalf("usage.json = %+v", cur)
	}
}
```
