Usage type, old:

```go
// storymakerNarratePayload is the payload of a narrate job.
```

new:

```go
const storymakerUsageVersion = 1

// storymakerUsageFile is usage.json: the characters sent to cloud voices on one local date.
type storymakerUsageFile struct {
	V     int    `json:"v"`
	Date  string `json:"date"`
	Chars int    `json:"chars"`
}

// storymakerNarrationUsage keeps the daily count in storymaker/narrate/usage.json. It fails closed:
// an unreadable file stops cloud calls for that date, because a budget guard that fails open would be
// a bypass.
type storymakerNarrationUsage struct {
	mu   sync.Mutex
	path string
	now  func() time.Time
}

func newStorymakerNarrationUsage(dir string) *storymakerNarrationUsage {
	return &storymakerNarrationUsage{path: filepath.Join(dir, "usage.json"), now: time.Now}
}

func (u *storymakerNarrationUsage) badPath() string {
	return filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(u.path), "usage.bad.json")
}

// loadLocked returns the count of the current local date. A missing file or an older date starts at
// 0. An unreadable file is renamed to usage.bad.json and replaced by one that holds maxDaily.
func (u *storymakerNarrationUsage) loadLocked(maxDaily int) (storymakerUsageFile, error) {
	today := u.now().Format(time.DateOnly)
	fresh := storymakerUsageFile{V: storymakerUsageVersion, Date: today}
	data, err := os.ReadFile(u.path)
	if errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
		return fresh, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return storymakerUsageFile{}, err
	}
	var cur storymakerUsageFile
	if json.Unmarshal(data, &cur) != nil || cur.V != storymakerUsageVersion || cur.Chars < 0 || !storymakerValidUsageDate(cur.Date) {
		_ = fileutil.Rename(u.path, u.badPath())
		fresh.Chars = maxDaily
		return fresh, u.writeLocked(fresh)
	}
	if cur.Date != today {
		return fresh, nil
	}
	return cur, nil
}

func storymakerValidUsageDate(date string) bool {
	_, err := time.Parse(time.DateOnly, date)
	return err == nil
}

// writeLocked publishes the file through a synced temp file and a rename. It takes no job context:
// the count must survive a revoked run.
func (u *storymakerNarrationUsage) writeLocked(cur storymakerUsageFile) error {
	data, err := json.Marshal(cur)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(u.path), 0o700); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return fileutil.WriteFileContext(context.Background(), u.path, data, 0o600)
}

// reserve counts chars for today and writes the file, or returns a budget_exceeded job error.
func (u *storymakerNarrationUsage) reserve(chars, maxDaily int) error {
	u.mu.Lock()
	defer u.mu.Unlock()
	cur, err := u.loadLocked(maxDaily)
	if err != nil {
		return render.Fail(render.CodeBudgetExceeded, "The daily count in %s could not be read or written, so cloud voices stop.", filepath.Base(u.path))
	}
	if cur.Chars+chars > maxDaily {
		if _, statErr := os.Stat(u.badPath()); statErr == nil && cur.Chars >= maxDaily {
			return render.Fail(render.CodeBudgetExceeded, "Cloud voices stop for today: the daily limit of %d characters is reached, and an unreadable count was kept as %s.", maxDaily, filepath.Base(u.badPath()))
		}
		return render.Fail(render.CodeBudgetExceeded, "The daily limit of %d characters for cloud voices is reached: %d are used and this piece needs %d.", maxDaily, cur.Chars, chars)
	}
	cur.Chars += chars
	if err := u.writeLocked(cur); err != nil {
		return render.Fail(render.CodeBudgetExceeded, "The daily count in %s could not be read or written, so cloud voices stop.", filepath.Base(u.path))
	}
	return nil
}

// release subtracts chars after a provider error with no bytes.
func (u *storymakerNarrationUsage) release(chars, maxDaily int) {
	u.mu.Lock()
	defer u.mu.Unlock()
	cur, err := u.loadLocked(maxDaily)
	if err != nil {
		return
	}
	cur.Chars -= chars
	if cur.Chars < 0 {
		cur.Chars = 0
	}
	_ = u.writeLocked(cur)
}

// today returns the characters counted for the current local date.
func (u *storymakerNarrationUsage) today(maxDaily int) (int, error) {
	u.mu.Lock()
	defer u.mu.Unlock()
	cur, err := u.loadLocked(maxDaily)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	return cur.Chars, nil
}

// storymakerNarratePayload is the payload of a narrate job.
```

```go
// storymakerClosedPort returns a local port nothing listens on.
func storymakerClosedPort(t *testing.T) int {
	t.Helper()
	l, err := net.Listen("tcp", "127.0.0.1:0")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	port := l.Addr().(*net.TCPAddr).Port
	if err := l.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return port
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `internal/server/storymaker_audio_handlers.go`:

```go
package server

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"aurago/internal/config"
	"aurago/internal/phonon"
	"aurago/internal/speechlab"
	"aurago/internal/storymaker"
	"aurago/internal/tools"
)

// storymakerVoiceListTimeout bounds one provider list, so a sidecar that hangs cannot hold GET /voices.
const storymakerVoiceListTimeout = 5 * time.Second

// storymakerPhononLanguages are the bases phonon.Language accepts (internal/phonon/catalog.go:63-70).
var storymakerPhononLanguages = []string{"de", "en", "es", "fr", "pt"}

// storymakerSanoLanguages are the languages sanotts.Voice has a voice for. hi, ne and zh fall back
// to English there (internal/sanotts/sanotts.go:46-65), so they are left out.
var storymakerSanoLanguages = []string{"ar", "cs", "de", "en", "es", "fr", "id", "it", "pt", "ro", "ru", "tr", "vi"}

// storymakerVoiceRow is the part of a row that every provider shares. Readiness runs on a copy of
// the config with this provider selected, on the tool route, because the chat route forces
// speech_lab (internal/tools/tts_config.go:35-37).
func storymakerVoiceRow(cfg *config.Config, id string) storymaker.VoiceProvider {
	cp := *cfg
	cp.TTS.Provider = id
	return storymaker.VoiceProvider{
		ID:              id,
		Ready:           tools.TTSReady(&cp, tools.TTSRouteTool),
		Languages:       []string{"*"},
		TestedLanguages: []string{},
		ChunkLimit:      storymaker.ChunkLimit(id),
	}
}

// storymakerBoundedList runs one provider list with the shared time limit. The list calls take no
// context, so a late answer is dropped and its goroutine ends with the call's own timeout.
func storymakerBoundedList[T any](ctx context.Context, list func() ([]T, error)) ([]T, error) {
	type answer struct {
		items []T
		err   error
	}
	done := make(chan answer, 1)
	go func() {
		items, err := list()
		done <- answer{items, err}
	}()
	timer := time.NewTimer(storymakerVoiceListTimeout)
	defer timer.Stop()
	select {
	case a := <-done:
		return a.items, a.err
	case <-timer.C:
		return nil, errors.New("the voice list timed out")
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return nil, ctx.Err()
	}
}

// storymakerPhononRule is the phonon readiness rule. TTSReady says yes for phonon with no files at
// all (internal/tools/tts_config.go:166-167), so the runtime and the voice must be checked as well.
func storymakerPhononRule(ttsReady bool, readyErr, voiceErr error) (ready, needsInstall bool) {
	needsInstall = readyErr != nil || voiceErr != nil
	return ttsReady && !needsInstall, needsInstall
}

func storymakerPhononRow(cfg *config.Config) storymaker.VoiceProvider {
	row := storymakerVoiceRow(cfg, "phonon")
	row.Languages = storymakerPhononLanguages
	root, dataDir := phonon.Root(), cfg.Directories.DataDir
	voice := strings.TrimSpace(cfg.TTS.Phonon.VoiceID)
	if voice == "" {
		voice = phonon.DefaultVoice(cfg.SanoTTSLanguage(""), cfg.Personality.CorePersonality)
	}
	if voice == "" {
		// The configured language is one phonon does not speak. English stands in, so the row
		// still tells whether phonon can speak at all.
		voice = phonon.DefaultVoice("en", cfg.Personality.CorePersonality)
	}
	row.DefaultVoice = voice
	_, voiceErr := phonon.VoicePath(root, dataDir, voice)
	row.Ready, row.NeedsInstall = storymakerPhononRule(row.Ready, phonon.Ready(root), voiceErr)
	if row.NeedsInstall {
		row.Note = "The Phonon runtime or the chosen voice is not installed."
	}
	for _, v := range phonon.Voices(root, dataDir) {
		installed := v.Installed
		row.Voices = append(row.Voices, storymaker.VoiceOption{ID: v.ID, Name: v.Name, Language: v.Language, Gender: v.Gender, Installed: &installed})
	}
	return row
}

// storymakerPiperLanguages lists the bases of the voice locales, sorted and without repeats.
func storymakerPiperLanguages(voices []tools.WyomingVoice) []string {
	seen := make(map[string]bool)
	out := []string{}
	for _, v := range voices {
		for _, locale := range v.Languages {
			base := storymaker.LanguageBase(locale)
			if base != "" && !seen[base] {
				seen[base] = true
				out = append(out, base)
			}
		}
	}
	sort.Strings(out)
	return out
}

func storymakerPiperRow(ctx context.Context, cfg *config.Config) storymaker.VoiceProvider {
	row := storymakerVoiceRow(cfg, "piper")
	// "piper" is the TTS system name, not a voice (internal/tools/tts.go:655-659).
	if voice := strings.TrimSpace(cfg.TTS.Piper.Voice); voice != "piper" {
		row.DefaultVoice = voice
	}
	if !row.Ready {
		return row
	}
	voices, err := storymakerBoundedList(ctx, func() ([]tools.WyomingVoice, error) {
		return tools.PiperListVoices(cfg.TTS.Piper.ContainerPort)
	})
	if err != nil {
		row.Ready = false
		row.Note = "The Piper voice list could not be read."
		return row
	}
	for _, v := range voices {
		installed := v.Installed
		option := storymaker.VoiceOption{ID: v.Name, Name: v.Name, Installed: &installed}
		if len(v.Languages) > 0 {
			option.Language = storymaker.LanguageBase(v.Languages[0])
		}
		row.Voices = append(row.Voices, option)
	}
	if languages := storymakerPiperLanguages(voices); len(languages) > 0 {
		row.Languages = languages
	}
	return row
}

func storymakerSanoRow(cfg *config.Config) storymaker.VoiceProvider {
	row := storymakerVoiceRow(cfg, "sanotts")
	row.Languages = storymakerSanoLanguages
	row.Note = "The voice follows the language. hi, ne and zh have no voice here, so they are not offered."
	return row
}

func storymakerSupertonicRow(ctx context.Context, cfg *config.Config) storymaker.VoiceProvider {
	row := storymakerVoiceRow(cfg, "supertonic")
	row.DefaultVoice = strings.TrimSpace(cfg.TTS.Supertonic.Voice)
	if row.DefaultVoice == "" {
		row.DefaultVoice = "M1" // the default of internal/tools/tts.go:679-682
	}
	if !row.Ready {
		return row
	}
	styles, err := storymakerBoundedList(ctx, func() ([]tools.SupertonicStyle, error) {
		return tools.SupertonicListStyles(cfg.TTS.Supertonic.URL)
	})
	if err != nil {
		row.Ready = false
		row.Note = "The Supertonic style list could not be read."
		return row
	}
	for _, style := range styles {
		row.Voices = append(row.Voices, storymaker.VoiceOption{ID: style.Name, Name: style.Name})
	}
	return row
}

// storymakerSpeechLabRow lists the one voice of the running stack. Speech Lab has no per-call voice,
// so the /ready snapshot is the whole list.
func (s *Server) storymakerSpeechLabRow(ctx context.Context, cfg *config.Config) storymaker.VoiceProvider {
	row := storymakerVoiceRow(cfg, "speech_lab")
	if base := storymaker.LanguageBase(cfg.SpeechLab.Language); base != "" && base != "auto" {
		row.Languages = []string{base}
	}
	if !row.Ready {
		return row
	}
	client := s.SpeechLab
	if client == nil {
		created, err := speechlab.NewClient(cfg.SpeechLab)
		if err != nil {
			row.Ready = false
			row.Note = "The Speech Lab configuration is not valid."
			return row
		}
		client = created
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, storymakerVoiceListTimeout)
	defer cancel()
	ready, err := client.Require(ctx, false, true)
	if err != nil {
		row.Ready = false
		row.Note = "Speech Lab reports no ready voice."
		return row
	}
	row.DefaultVoice = ready.Voice
	row.Voices = []storymaker.VoiceOption{{ID: ready.Voice, Name: ready.Voice}}
	row.Note = "The voice comes from the running Speech Lab stack."
	return row
}

// storymakerLocalVoiceRows returns the local providers in the order of the provider list.
func (s *Server) storymakerLocalVoiceRows(ctx context.Context, cfg *config.Config) []storymaker.VoiceProvider {
	return []storymaker.VoiceProvider{
		storymakerPhononRow(cfg),
		storymakerPiperRow(ctx, cfg),
		storymakerSanoRow(cfg),
		storymakerSupertonicRow(ctx, cfg),
		s.storymakerSpeechLabRow(ctx, cfg),
	}
}
```

```go
// handleStorymakerVoices answers GET /voices. It works while the desktop is read-only.
func handleStorymakerVoices(s *Server, w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodGet {
		writeStorymakerMethodError(w, "GET")
		return
	}
	if _, ok := storymakerAuthorize(s, w, r, desktopScopeRead, desktopRead, false); !ok {
		return
	}
	writeStorymakerJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]any{"providers": s.storymakerVoiceProviders(r.Context())})
}

// storymakerCloudVoiceIDs are the cloud ids, sorted. google joins only when it is the active provider.
var storymakerCloudVoiceIDs = []string{"elevenlabs", "google", "minimax", "mistral", "openai", "resemble", "stepfun", "xai"}

// storymakerXAIVoiceTTL is how long a fetched xAI list stays valid.
const storymakerXAIVoiceTTL = 5 * time.Minute

// storymakerVoiceCache holds the xAI voice list, the only list that costs a network call to a
// provider. A failed fetch is cached too, so the voice panel cannot hammer the endpoint.
type storymakerVoiceCache struct {
	mu      sync.Mutex
	client  *realtimespeech.Client
	now     func() time.Time
	key     string
	fetched time.Time
	voices  []realtimespeech.Voice
	err     error
}

func newStorymakerVoiceCache() *storymakerVoiceCache {
	return &storymakerVoiceCache{client: realtimespeech.NewClient(), now: time.Now}
}

// xai returns the list for this API key. A caller that went away does not fill the cache, because
// its cancelled request says nothing about xAI.
func (c *storymakerVoiceCache) xai(ctx context.Context, apiKey string) ([]realtimespeech.Voice, error) {
	if c == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("the voice cache is not available")
	}
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	if c.key == apiKey && !c.fetched.IsZero() && c.now().Sub(c.fetched) < storymakerXAIVoiceTTL {
		return c.voices, c.err
	}
	fetchCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, storymakerVoiceListTimeout)
	defer cancel()
	voices, err := c.client.FetchXAIVoices(fetchCtx, apiKey)
	if err != nil && ctx.Err() != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	c.key, c.fetched, c.voices, c.err = apiKey, c.now(), voices, err
	return voices, err
}

// storymakerConfiguredVoice is the configured voice, or the provider's built-in default.
func storymakerConfiguredVoice(configured, fallback string) string {
	if voice := strings.TrimSpace(configured); voice != "" {
		return voice
	}
	return fallback
}

// storymakerCloudVoiceRow builds one cloud row. The fallbacks are the defaults of the synthesis code
// in internal/tools (tts.go:478, tts.go:793, tts_cloud.go:21-24, tts_cloud.go:37).
func storymakerCloudVoiceRow(ctx context.Context, cfg *config.Config, id string, cache *storymakerVoiceCache) storymaker.VoiceProvider {
	row := storymakerVoiceRow(cfg, id)
	switch id {
	case "elevenlabs":
		row.FreeText, row.DefaultVoice = true, storymakerConfiguredVoice(cfg.TTS.ElevenLabs.VoiceID, "21m00Tcm4TlvDq8ikWAM")
	case "minimax":
		row.FreeText, row.DefaultVoice = true, storymakerConfiguredVoice(cfg.TTS.MiniMax.VoiceID, "English_expressive_narrator")
	case "mistral":
		row.FreeText, row.DefaultVoice = true, storymakerConfiguredVoice(cfg.TTS.Mistral.VoiceID, "")
		if !row.Ready {
			row.Note = "Needs an API key and a voice id."
		}
	case "resemble":
		row.FreeText, row.DefaultVoice = true, storymakerConfiguredVoice(cfg.TTS.Resemble.VoiceID, "")
		if !row.Ready {
			row.Note = "Needs an API key and a voice id."
		}
	case "stepfun":
		row.FreeText, row.DefaultVoice = true, storymakerConfiguredVoice(cfg.TTS.StepFun.VoiceID, "jingdiannvsheng")
	case "openai":
		row.DefaultVoice = storymakerConfiguredVoice(cfg.TTS.OpenAI.VoiceID, "coral")
		for _, voice := range tools.OpenAITTSVoices() {
			row.Voices = append(row.Voices, storymaker.VoiceOption{ID: voice, Name: voice})
		}
		row.Note = "The default model gpt-4o-mini-tts shuts down on 2027-01-06."
	case "xai":
		row.DefaultVoice = storymakerConfiguredVoice(cfg.TTS.XAI.VoiceID, "eve")
		if !row.Ready {
			break
		}
		voices, err := cache.xai(ctx, strings.TrimSpace(cfg.TTS.XAI.APIKey))
		if err != nil {
			row.Ready = false
			row.Note = "The xAI voice list could not be read."
			break
		}
		for _, voice := range voices {
			row.Voices = append(row.Voices, storymaker.VoiceOption{ID: voice.ID, Name: voice.Label})
		}
	case "google":
		row.Note = "Language only, with no voice choice. It uses an unofficial endpoint."
	}
	if !row.Ready && row.Note == "" {
		row.Note = "Needs an API key."
	}
	return row
}

// storymakerActiveVoiceProvider is the provider the tool route would use, as a list id.
func storymakerActiveVoiceProvider(cfg *config.Config) string {
	id := storymaker.NormalizeProvider(tools.BuildTTSConfig(cfg, tools.TTSRouteTool, "").Provider)
	if id == "s2s" || id == "s2s-lab" {
		return "speech_lab"
	}
	return id
}

// storymakerVoiceProviders is the one provider list behind GET /voices, POST /projects and
// POST /narrate: the active provider first, then the local ids, then the cloud ids sorted. Keeping
// local ahead of cloud means voice resolution never picks a paid provider ahead of a free one.
func (s *Server) storymakerVoiceProviders(ctx context.Context) []storymaker.VoiceProvider {
	cfg := s.ConfigSnapshot()
	if cfg == nil {
		return []storymaker.VoiceProvider{}
	}
	var cache *storymakerVoiceCache
	if rt, err := s.storymakerRuntime(); err == nil {
		cache = rt.voices
	}
	active := storymakerActiveVoiceProvider(cfg)
	rows := s.storymakerLocalVoiceRows(ctx, cfg)
	for _, id := range storymakerCloudVoiceIDs {
		if id == "google" && active != "google" {
			continue
		}
		rows = append(rows, storymakerCloudVoiceRow(ctx, cfg, id, cache))
	}
	out := make([]storymaker.VoiceProvider, 0, len(rows))
	for _, row := range rows {
		if row.ID == active {
			row.Active = true
			out = append(out, row)
		}
	}
	for _, row := range rows {
		if row.ID != active {
			out = append(out, row)
		}
	}
	return out
}
```

```go
// storymakerAudioProject makes a project whose story has this narration and voice.
func storymakerAudioProject(t *testing.T, s *Server, token, narration string, voice *storymaker.Voice) string {
	t.Helper()
	p := createStorymakerProject(t, s, token)
	story := p.Story
	story.Voice = voice
	story.Scenes[0].Narration = narration
	data, err := storymaker.CanonicalJSON(story)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	w := storymakerRequest(s, token, http.MethodPut, "/projects/"+p.Project.ID+"/story", bytes.NewReader(data), map[string]string{"If-Match": p.ETag})
	if w.Code != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("save story = %d %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
	}
	return p.Project.ID
}

func TestStorymakerNarrationAnswer(t *testing.T) {
	s, readToken, writeToken := storymakerAPIFixture(t)
	id := storymakerAudioProject(t, s, writeToken, "This is a short story about a firewall.", &storymaker.Voice{Provider: "sanotts", Language: "de"})

	w := storymakerRequest(s, readToken, http.MethodGet, "/projects/"+id+"/narration", nil, nil)
	if w.Code != http.StatusOK || w.Header().Get("Cache-Control") != "no-store" {
		t.Fatalf("narration = %d %q %s", w.Code, w.Header().Get("Cache-Control"), w.Body.String())
	}
	var got storymaker.AudioAnswer
	decodeBody(t, w, &got)
	// The text is one sentence of 39 code points (8 words, width 39, so nothing merges or splits) and
	// nothing is stored yet: one missing block, an empty narration map and no music.
	if len(got.Missing) != 1 || got.Missing[0].BlockID != "s1:n" || got.Missing[0].Chars != 39 || got.Chars.Total != 39 || got.Chars.Missing != 39 {
		t.Errorf("missing = %+v, chars = %+v", got.Missing, got.Chars)
	}
	if got.Narration == nil || len(got.Narration) != 0 || got.Music != nil || got.MusicIssue != "" {
		t.Errorf("answer = %s", w.Body.String())
	}

	unknown := storymakerRequest(s, readToken, http.MethodGet, "/projects/33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333333/narration", nil, nil)
	if unknown.Code != http.StatusNotFound || !strings.Contains(unknown.Body.String(), `"code":"project_not_found"`) {
		t.Errorf("unknown project = %d %s", unknown.Code, unknown.Body.String())
	}

	// A story.json damaged by a hand edit.
	file := filepath.Join(s.ConfigSnapshot().VirtualDesktop.WorkspaceDir, "Documents", "Storymaker", id, "story.json")
	if err := os.WriteFile(file, []byte(`{"schema": "storymaker/1", "scenes": [`), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, tc := range []struct{ method, path string }{
		{http.MethodGet, "/projects/" + id + "/narration"},
		{http.MethodPost, "/projects/" + id + "/narrate"},
	} {
		w := storymakerRequest(s, writeToken, tc.method, tc.path, nil, nil)
		if w.Code != http.StatusUnprocessableEntity || !strings.Contains(w.Body.String(), `"code":"invalid_story"`) {
			t.Errorf("damaged story %s %s = %d %s", tc.method, tc.path, w.Code, w.Body.String())
		}
	}
}

// A Phonon voice that is not installed fails in the job, so the route refuses it first and the alternatives never name it.
// Settings offers every Piper voice, so that flag decides nothing.
func TestStorymakerVoiceListCheckKnowsInstalledPhononVoices(t *testing.T) {
	yes, no := true, false
	voices := []storymaker.VoiceOption{
		{ID: "absent", Language: "fr", Installed: &no},
		{ID: "there", Language: "fr", Installed: &yes},
		{ID: "unsaid", Language: "fr"},
	}
	phononRow := storymaker.VoiceProvider{ID: "phonon", Voices: voices}
	for voice, want := range map[string]bool{"absent": false, "there": true, "unsaid": true, "": true, "nobody": false} {
		if got := storymakerVoiceOnList(phononRow, voice); got != want {
			t.Errorf("phonon voice %q on the list = %v, want %v", voice, got, want)
		}
	}
	if got := storymakerVoiceAlternatives(phononRow, "fr"); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"there", "unsaid"}) {
		t.Errorf("phonon alternatives = %v", got)
	}
	piperRow := storymaker.VoiceProvider{ID: "piper", Voices: voices}
	if !storymakerVoiceOnList(piperRow, "absent") {
		t.Error("the piper flag refused a voice")
	}
	if got := storymakerVoiceAlternatives(piperRow, "fr"); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"absent", "there", "unsaid"}) {
		t.Errorf("piper alternatives = %v", got)
	}
}

func TestStorymakerNarrateQueuesOneJob(t *testing.T) {
	s, _, writeToken := storymakerAPIFixture(t)
	release := make(chan struct{})
	// Registered after the fixture's cleanup, so it runs first and the manager can close.
	t.Cleanup(func() { close(release) })
	s.storymakerAudioRunOverride = func(ctx context.Context, job render.Job, payload any, h *render.Handle) (render.Output, error) {
		select {
		case <-release:
		case <-ctx.Done():
		}
		return render.Output{}, nil
	}
	post := func(id string) (*httptest.ResponseRecorder, *render.Job) {
		t.Helper()
		w := storymakerRequest(s, writeToken, http.MethodPost, "/projects/"+id+"/narrate", nil, nil)
		var body struct {
			Job *render.Job `json:"job"`
		}
		decodeBody(t, w, &body)
		return w, body.Job
	}

	id := storymakerAudioProject(t, s, writeToken, "This is a short story about a firewall.", &storymaker.Voice{Provider: "sanotts", Language: "de"})
	w, first := post(id)
	if w.Code != http.StatusAccepted || first == nil || first.Kind != render.KindNarrate || first.ProjectID != id {
		t.Fatalf("new job = %d %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
	}
	w, second := post(id)
	if w.Code != http.StatusOK || second == nil || second.ID != first.ID {
		t.Fatalf("existing job = %d %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
	}

	// A story with nothing to say has nothing missing.
	empty := createStorymakerProject(t, s, writeToken).Project.ID
	w, none := post(empty)
	if w.Code != http.StatusOK || none != nil || !strings.Contains(w.Body.String(), `"job":null`) {
		t.Fatalf("nothing missing = %d %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
	}
}

func TestStorymakerNarrateRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	t.Setenv("AURAGO_PHONON_DIR", t.TempDir())
	s, _, writeToken := storymakerAPIFixture(t)
	s.Cfg.TTS.OpenAI.APIKey = "sk-fixture"

	type refusal struct {
		Code         string   `json:"code"`
		Message      string   `json:"message"`
		Alternatives []string `json:"alternatives"`
		Limit        int      `json:"limit"`
		Missing      int      `json:"missing"`
	}
	refuse := func(t *testing.T, narration string, voice *storymaker.Voice) refusal {
		t.Helper()
		id := storymakerAudioProject(t, s, writeToken, narration, voice)
		w := storymakerRequest(s, writeToken, http.MethodPost, "/projects/"+id+"/narrate", nil, nil)
		if w.Code != http.StatusConflict {
			t.Fatalf("narrate = %d %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
		}
		var got refusal
		decodeBody(t, w, &got)
		return got
	}
	const short = "This is a short story about a firewall."
	// One sentence of 151 code points: under the 400-rune limit of openai and alone in its block, so
	// it stays one piece and missing counts all 151.
	long := strings.Repeat("a", 150) + "."

	t.Run("no voice names the first block", func(t *testing.T) {
		got := refuse(t, short, nil)
		if got.Code != "voice_required" || !strings.Contains(got.Message, "s1:n") {
			t.Errorf("refusal = %+v", got)
		}
	})
	t.Run("provider not ready", func(t *testing.T) {
		// Piper is switched off in this config, so TTSReady says no.
		got := refuse(t, short, &storymaker.Voice{Provider: "piper", Voice: "de_DE-thorsten-high", Language: "de"})
		if got.Code != "narration_unavailable" || !strings.Contains(got.Message, "piper") {
			t.Errorf("refusal = %+v", got)
		}
	})
	t.Run("inactive google", func(t *testing.T) {
		got := refuse(t, short, &storymaker.Voice{Provider: "google", Language: "de"})
		if got.Code != "narration_unavailable" || !strings.Contains(got.Message, "google") {
			t.Errorf("refusal = %+v", got)
		}
	})
	t.Run("voice not on the list", func(t *testing.T) {
		got := refuse(t, short, &storymaker.Voice{Provider: "openai", Voice: "nobody", Language: "en"})
		// The openai list has 13 voices and the answer is capped at 5.
		if got.Code != "voice_unknown" || !strings.Contains(got.Message, "s1:n") || len(got.Alternatives) != 5 {
			t.Fatalf("refusal = %+v", got)
		}
		for _, voice := range got.Alternatives {
			if !slices.Contains(tools.OpenAITTSVoices(), voice) {
				t.Errorf("alternative %q is not an openai voice", voice)
			}
		}
	})
	t.Run("job cap", func(t *testing.T) {
		s.Cfg.Storymaker.Narration.MaxJobChars = 100
		got := refuse(t, long, &storymaker.Voice{Provider: "openai", Voice: "alloy", Language: "en"})
		if got.Code != "budget_exceeded" || got.Limit != 100 || got.Missing != 151 {
			t.Errorf("refusal = %+v", got)
		}
	})
	t.Run("daily cap", func(t *testing.T) {
		s.Cfg.Storymaker.Narration.MaxJobChars = 20000
		s.Cfg.Storymaker.Narration.MaxDailyChars = 1000
		rt, err := s.storymakerRuntime()
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		dir := filepath.Join(storymakerDir(rt.dataDir), "narrate")
		if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o700); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		usage := fmt.Sprintf(`{"v":1,"date":%q,"chars":950}`, time.Now().Format("2006-01-02"))
		if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "usage.json"), []byte(usage), 0o600); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		// 950 used today plus 151 missing is 1101, over the daily 1000. The job cap of 20000 is not reached.
		got := refuse(t, long, &storymaker.Voice{Provider: "openai", Voice: "alloy", Language: "en"})
		if got.Code != "budget_exceeded" || got.Limit != 1000 || got.Missing != 151 {
			t.Errorf("refusal = %+v", got)
		}
		// A damaged file is a full count, so the route refuses as the job would.
		if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "usage.json"), []byte(`{"chars":`), 0o600); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		got = refuse(t, short, &storymaker.Voice{Provider: "openai", Voice: "alloy", Language: "en"})
		if got.Code != "budget_exceeded" || got.Limit != 1000 || got.Missing != 39 {
			t.Errorf("refusal with a damaged file = %+v", got)
		}
	})
	t.Run("read-only", func(t *testing.T) {
		id := storymakerAudioProject(t, s, writeToken, short, &storymaker.Voice{Provider: "sanotts", Language: "de"})
		ro := *s.ConfigSnapshot()
		ro.VirtualDesktop.ReadOnly = true
		s.replaceConfigSnapshot(&ro)
		w := storymakerRequest(s, writeToken, http.MethodPost, "/projects/"+id+"/narrate", nil, nil)
		if w.Code != http.StatusForbidden || !strings.Contains(w.Body.String(), "desktop_readonly") {
			t.Errorf("read-only narrate = %d %s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
		}
	})
}
```

```go
// storymakerCodeVoiceUnknown answers a voice that is not on its provider's list or cannot run here.
const storymakerCodeVoiceUnknown = "voice_unknown"

// storymakerMaxVoiceAlternatives is the cap of contract line 60.
const storymakerMaxVoiceAlternatives = 5

// writeStorymakerErrorWith is writeStorymakerError with extra fields next to the fixed three.
func writeStorymakerErrorWith(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, code, message string, extra map[string]any) {
	body := make(map[string]any, len(extra)+3)
	for key, value := range extra {
		body[key] = value
	}
	body["error"], body["code"], body["message"] = code, code, message
	writeStorymakerJSON(w, status, body)
}

// storymakerMusicKey resolves the key of the story's music. Batch 2B has no music, so nothing is to
// be played. A15 writes the full rule.
func (s *Server) storymakerMusicKey(ctx context.Context, m *storymaker.Music) (string, error) {
	return "", nil
}

// handleStorymakerNarration answers GET /projects/{id}/narration. It works while the desktop is read-only.
func handleStorymakerNarration(s *Server, w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, id string) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodGet {
		writeStorymakerMethodError(w, "GET")
		return
	}
	acc, ok := storymakerAuthorize(s, w, r, desktopScopeRead, desktopRead, true)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	_, answer, ok := s.storymakerStoryAudio(w, r, acc, id)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	writeStorymakerJSON(w, http.StatusOK, answer)
}

// handleStorymakerNarrate answers POST /projects/{id}/narrate: 200 {job:null} when nothing is
// missing, the refusals in their fixed order, then 202 for a new job and 200 for the job of this
// project that is already queued or running.
func handleStorymakerNarrate(s *Server, w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, id string) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodPost {
		writeStorymakerMethodError(w, "POST")
		return
	}
	acc, ok := storymakerAuthorize(s, w, r, desktopScopeWrite, desktopWrite, true)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	story, answer, ok := s.storymakerStoryAudio(w, r, acc, id)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	if len(answer.Missing) == 0 {
		writeStorymakerJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]any{"job": nil})
		return
	}
	if storymakerNarrateRefused(r.Context(), w, s, acc, storymakerBlocksToSynthesize(story, answer), answer) {
		return
	}
	job, replayed, err := acc.rt.narrate.Enqueue(render.EnqueueRequest{
		ProjectID: id,
		Kind:      render.KindNarrate,
		Coalesce:  true,
		Payload:   storymakerNarratePayload{ProjectID: id},
	})
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, render.ErrBusy):
		writeStorymakerError(w, http.StatusTooManyRequests, render.CodeBusy, "128 narration jobs are already waiting. Try again when one has finished.", nil)
	case err != nil:
		writeStorymakerError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "job_failed", "The narration job could not be queued.", nil)
	case replayed:
		writeStorymakerJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]any{"job": job})
	default:
		writeStorymakerJSON(w, http.StatusAccepted, map[string]any{"job": job})
	}
}

// storymakerStoryAudio reads the story with its defaults applied, the way a render job sees it, and
// its audio answer. On a failure it has answered the request and returns false.
func (s *Server) storymakerStoryAudio(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, acc *storymakerAccess, id string) (*storymaker.Story, storymaker.AudioAnswer, bool) {
	ctx := r.Context()
	data, _, err := acc.rt.projects.LoadStory(ctx, id)
	if err != nil {
		mapStorymakerError(w, err, "project_not_found")
		return nil, storymaker.AudioAnswer{}, false
	}
	story, _, err := storymaker.DecodeStory(data, storymakerLimits(acc.cfg))
	if err != nil {
		mapStorymakerError(w, err, "project_not_found")
		return nil, storymaker.AudioAnswer{}, false
	}
	storymaker.ApplyDefaults(&story)
	answer, err := acc.rt.projects.AudioAnswer(ctx, id, &story, s.storymakerMusicKey)
	if err != nil {
		mapStorymakerError(w, err, "project_not_found")
		return nil, storymaker.AudioAnswer{}, false
	}
	return &story, answer, true
}

// storymakerBlocksToSynthesize keeps the blocks with missing pieces. Only they call a provider, so
// a provider that went away cannot block a story whose other blocks need it no more.
func storymakerBlocksToSynthesize(story *storymaker.Story, answer storymaker.AudioAnswer) []storymaker.NarrationBlock {
	missing := make(map[string]bool, len(answer.Missing))
	for _, m := range answer.Missing {
		missing[m.BlockID] = true
	}
	var out []storymaker.NarrationBlock
	for _, b := range storymaker.Blocks(story) {
		if missing[b.ID] {
			out = append(out, b)
		}
	}
	return out
}

// storymakerNarrateRefused answers the first refusal in the order voice_required,
// narration_unavailable, voice_unknown, budget_exceeded, and reports whether it answered.
func storymakerNarrateRefused(ctx context.Context, w http.ResponseWriter, s *Server, acc *storymakerAccess, blocks []storymaker.NarrationBlock, answer storymaker.AudioAnswer) bool {
	for _, b := range blocks {
		if b.Voice.Provider == "" {
			writeStorymakerError(w, http.StatusConflict, render.CodeVoiceRequired, fmt.Sprintf("Block %s has no voice. Choose a voice for the story first.", b.ID), nil)
			return true
		}
	}
	rows := make(map[string]storymaker.VoiceProvider)
	for _, row := range s.storymakerVoiceProviders(ctx) {
		rows[row.ID] = row
	}
	for _, b := range blocks {
		row, offered := rows[b.Voice.Provider]
		if !offered {
			writeStorymakerError(w, http.StatusConflict, render.CodeNarrationUnavailable, fmt.Sprintf("The voice provider %s is not offered here. Block %s needs it.", b.Voice.Provider, b.ID), nil)
			return true
		}
		if !row.Ready {
			writeStorymakerError(w, http.StatusConflict, render.CodeNarrationUnavailable, fmt.Sprintf("The voice provider %s is not ready. Block %s needs it.", b.Voice.Provider, b.ID), nil)
			return true
		}
	}
	for _, b := range blocks {
		row := rows[b.Voice.Provider]
		if !storymakerVoiceOnList(row, b.Voice.Voice) {
			writeStorymakerErrorWith(w, http.StatusConflict, storymakerCodeVoiceUnknown,
				fmt.Sprintf("The voice %s of %s is not available here. Block %s uses it.", b.Voice.Voice, b.Voice.Provider, b.ID),
				map[string]any{"alternatives": storymakerVoiceAlternatives(row, b.Voice.Language)})
			return true
		}
	}
	cloud := make(map[string]bool, len(blocks))
	for _, b := range blocks {
		cloud[b.ID] = !storymaker.LocalVoiceProvider(b.Voice.Provider)
	}
	chars := 0
	for _, m := range answer.Missing {
		if cloud[m.BlockID] {
			chars += m.Chars
		}
	}
	if chars == 0 {
		return false
	}
	caps := acc.cfg.Storymaker.Narration
	// today turns a damaged file into a full count, so the route refuses as the job would. A read
	// error counts as 0 here, because the job loop holds the real guard.
	used, err := acc.rt.usage.today(caps.MaxDailyChars)
	if err != nil {
		used = 0
	}
	var limit int
	switch {
	case chars > caps.MaxJobChars:
		limit = caps.MaxJobChars
	case used+chars > caps.MaxDailyChars:
		limit = caps.MaxDailyChars
	default:
		return false
	}
	writeStorymakerErrorWith(w, http.StatusConflict, render.CodeBudgetExceeded,
		fmt.Sprintf("This narration needs %d characters from a paid provider and the limit is %d.", chars, limit),
		map[string]any{"limit": limit, "missing": chars})
	return true
}

// storymakerVoiceOnList is the list check of POST /narrate. An empty id means the provider's
// default, and a free-text provider or one without a list has nothing to check against. A listed
// voice that cannot run counts as off the list, so the refusal comes before the job.
func storymakerVoiceOnList(row storymaker.VoiceProvider, voice string) bool {
	if voice == "" || row.FreeText || len(row.Voices) == 0 {
		return true
	}
	for _, option := range row.Voices {
		if option.ID == voice {
			return storymakerVoiceUsable(row, option)
		}
	}
	return false
}

// storymakerVoiceUsable says whether a listed voice can run. A Phonon voice that is not installed
// fails in the job, because nothing downloads it on the way (phonon.VoicePath). Settings offers every
// Piper voice (ui/cfg/tts.js:593-601), so that flag decides nothing.
func storymakerVoiceUsable(row storymaker.VoiceProvider, option storymaker.VoiceOption) bool {
	return row.ID != "phonon" || option.Installed == nil || *option.Installed
}

// storymakerVoiceAlternatives names at most five voices of the provider, those of the language first.
func storymakerVoiceAlternatives(row storymaker.VoiceProvider, language string) []string {
	base := storymaker.LanguageBase(language)
	out := make([]string, 0, storymakerMaxVoiceAlternatives)
	for _, sameLanguage := range []bool{true, false} {
		for _, option := range row.Voices {
			if len(out) == storymakerMaxVoiceAlternatives {
				return out
			}
			if storymakerVoiceUsable(row, option) && (storymaker.LanguageBase(option.Language) == base) == sameLanguage {
				out = append(out, option.ID)
			}
		}
	}
	return out
}
```

```go
	}
	env := storymaker.Envelope{V: 1, DurationMS: 100, Rate: 100, Levels: make(storymaker.Levels, 10)}
	if err := rt.projects.WritePiece(ctx, id, hash, "wav", data, env); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	folder := filepath.Join(s.ConfigSnapshot().VirtualDesktop.WorkspaceDir, "Documents", "Storymaker", id, "audio", "narration")
	base := "/projects/" + id + "/audio/"

	// The whole file, with the fixed headers of the route.
	w := storymakerRequest(s, readToken, http.MethodGet, base+"narration/"+hash+".wav", nil, nil)
	if w.Code != http.StatusOK || !bytes.Equal(w.Body.Bytes(), data) {
		t.Fatalf("full GET = %d, %d bytes", w.Code, w.Body.Len())
	}
	for header, want := range map[string]string{
		"Content-Type":            "audio/wav",
		"X-Content-Type-Options":  "nosniff",
		"Content-Security-Policy": "sandbox; default-src 'none'",
		"Cache-Control":           "private, max-age=86400, immutable",
	} {
		if got := w.Header().Get(header); got != want {
			t.Errorf("%s = %q, want %q", header, got, want)
		}
	}

	// A range.
	w = storymakerRequest(s, readToken, http.MethodGet, base+"narration/"+hash+".wav", nil, map[string]string{"Range": "bytes=10-19"})
	if w.Code != http.StatusPartialContent || !bytes.Equal(w.Body.Bytes(), data[10:20]) || w.Header().Get("Content-Range") != "bytes 10-19/1000" {
		t.Errorf("range GET = %d %q, %d bytes", w.Code, w.Header().Get("Content-Range"), w.Body.Len())
	}

	// HEAD sends the length and no body.
	w = storymakerRequest(s, readToken, http.MethodHead, base+"narration/"+hash+".wav", nil, nil)
	if w.Code != http.StatusOK || w.Body.Len() != 0 || w.Header().Get("Content-Length") != "1000" {
		t.Errorf("HEAD = %d, %d bytes, length %q", w.Code, w.Body.Len(), w.Header().Get("Content-Length"))
	}

	// A file over the 50 MB limit of Store.ReadFile still serves, through Store.Open.
	big := strings.Repeat("c", 64)
	f, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(folder, big+".mp3"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := f.Truncate(51 << 20); err != nil { // 51 MiB is 53477376 bytes
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := f.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, _, err := rt.store.ReadFile(ctx, "Documents/Storymaker/"+id+"/audio/narration/"+big+".mp3"); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("ReadFile took the big file, so this case proves nothing")
	}
	// The last 6 bytes: 53477376 - 6 = 53477370.
	w = storymakerRequest(s, readToken, http.MethodGet, base+"narration/"+big+".mp3", nil, map[string]string{"Range": "bytes=53477370-53477375"})
	if w.Code != http.StatusPartialContent || w.Body.Len() != 6 || w.Header().Get("Content-Type") != "audio/mpeg" {
		t.Errorf("big file = %d, %d bytes, %q", w.Code, w.Body.Len(), w.Header().Get("Content-Type"))
	}

	// Only wav and mp3 files with a lower-case hash name, in two folders. The envelope exists on
	// disk, so its 404 comes from the route rule.
	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(folder, hash+".env.json")); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, path := range []string{
		"exports/" + hash + ".wav",
		"narration/" + hash + ".env.json",
		"narration/" + hash + ".meta.json",
		"narration/" + hash + ".pending.json",
		"narration/" + strings.ToUpper(hash) + ".wav",
		"narration/" + hash + ".ogg",
		"narration/" + strings.Repeat("b", 64) + ".wav",
		"music/" + hash + ".mp3",
	} {
		w := storymakerRequest(s, readToken, http.MethodGet, base+path, nil, nil)
		if w.Code != http.StatusNotFound || !strings.Contains(w.Body.String(), `"code":"audio_not_found"`) {
			t.Errorf("%s = %d %s", path, w.Code, w.Body.String())
		}
	}

	w = storymakerRequest(s, writeToken, http.MethodPost, base+"narration/"+hash+".wav", nil, nil)
	if w.Code != http.StatusMethodNotAllowed || w.Header().Get("Allow") != "GET, HEAD" {
		t.Errorf("POST = %d, Allow %q", w.Code, w.Header().Get("Allow"))
	}
}

func TestStorymakerNarrationAnswer(t *testing.T) {
```

In the same file, replace the line `// writeStorymakerErrorWith is writeStorymakerError with extra fields next to the fixed three.` with:

```go
// storymakerAudioFilePattern is the only file name the audio routes serve. By construction it leaves
// out env.json, meta.json and pending.json.
var storymakerAudioFilePattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^([0-9a-f]{64})\.(wav|mp3)$`)

// storymakerAudioTypes gives each served extension its fixed Content-Type.
var storymakerAudioTypes = map[string]string{"wav": "audio/wav", "mp3": "audio/mpeg"}

// handleStorymakerAudio serves GET and HEAD /projects/{id}/audio/{narration|music}/{name}. These are
// inert files: nosniff and a sandbox stop a browser that navigates to the URL from running anything.
// It goes through Store.Open, because Store.ReadFile stops at 50 MB and music can be larger.
func handleStorymakerAudio(s *Server, w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, id, folder, file string) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodGet && r.Method != http.MethodHead {
		writeStorymakerMethodError(w, "GET, HEAD")
		return
	}
	acc, ok := storymakerAuthorize(s, w, r, desktopScopeRead, desktopRead, true)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	match := storymakerAudioFilePattern.FindStringSubmatch(file)
	if (folder != "narration" && folder != "music") || match == nil {
		writeStorymakerError(w, http.StatusNotFound, "audio_not_found", "Audio file not found.", nil)
		return
	}
	content, info, err := acc.rt.projects.OpenAudio(r.Context(), id, folder, file)
	if err != nil {
		mapStorymakerError(w, err, "audio_not_found")
		return
	}
	defer content.Close()
	h := w.Header()
	h.Set("Content-Type", storymakerAudioTypes[match[2]])
	h.Set("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
	h.Set("Content-Security-Policy", "sandbox; default-src 'none'")
	h.Set("Cache-Control", "private, max-age=86400, immutable")
	http.ServeContent(w, r, file, info.ModTime, content)
}

// writeStorymakerErrorWith is writeStorymakerError with extra fields next to the fixed three.
```

```go
// storymakerDefaultVoiceFor is the default voice of a provider for a language, for
// ResolveProjectVoice. A list voice of that language wins, the provider's own default first, so
// piper does not start a German story with a voice of another language.
func storymakerDefaultVoiceFor(cfg *config.Config, providers []storymaker.VoiceProvider) func(provider, language string) string {
	return func(provider, language string) string {
		id := storymaker.NormalizeProvider(provider)
		if id == "phonon" {
			return phonon.DefaultVoice(language, cfg.Personality.CorePersonality)
		}
		base := storymaker.LanguageBase(language)
		for _, row := range providers {
			if row.ID != id {
				continue
			}
			first := ""
			for _, option := range row.Voices {
				if storymaker.LanguageBase(option.Language) != base {
					continue
				}
				if option.ID == row.DefaultVoice {
					return option.ID
				}
				if first == "" {
					first = option.ID
				}
			}
			if first != "" {
				return first
			}
			return row.DefaultVoice
		}
		return ""
	}
}

type storymakerCreateRequest struct {
	Title    string `json:"title"`
	Aspect   string `json:"aspect"`
	FPS      int    `json:"fps"`
	Language string `json:"language"`
}
```

New:
```go
func bootToken(t *testing.T, fx *listenerFixture) string {
	t.Helper()
	res, body := fx.do(t, http.MethodGet, fx.l.Base()+"/boot.json", nil, nil)
	if res.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("boot.json = %d", res.StatusCode)
	}
	var doc map[string]any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(body, &doc); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	token, _ := doc["token"].(string)
	return token
}

func TestListenerServesTheTokenOnTheFirstGetOnly(t *testing.T) {
	fx := newListenerFixture(t)
	if head, _ := fx.do(t, http.MethodHead, fx.l.Base()+"/boot.json", nil, nil); head.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("HEAD boot.json = %d", head.StatusCode)
	}
	if got := bootToken(t, fx); got != testRenderToken {
		t.Fatalf("first GET after a HEAD: token = %q", got)
	}
	if got := bootToken(t, fx); got != "" {
		t.Fatalf("second GET: token = %q", got)
	}
}

func TestListenerRefusesARequestWithoutTheToken(t *testing.T) {
	fx := newListenerFixture(t)
	base := fx.l.Base()
	jsonHeader := map[string]string{"Content-Type": "application/json"}
	for _, q := range []string{"", "?t=", "?t=" + strings.Repeat("0", 32), "?t=" + testRenderToken[:31]} {
		res, body := fx.do(t, http.MethodPost, base+"/report"+q, []byte(`{"frame_count":3}`), jsonHeader)
		if res.StatusCode != http.StatusForbidden || string(body) != `{"error":"bad_token"}` {
			t.Errorf("report %q = %d %s", q, res.StatusCode, body)
		}
		res, body = fx.do(t, http.MethodPost, base+"/frame"+q, []byte("f"), map[string]string{"Content-Type": "text/plain", "X-SM-Frame": "0"})
		if res.StatusCode != http.StatusForbidden || string(body) != `{"stop":true,"error":"bad_token"}` {
			t.Errorf("frame %q = %d %s", q, res.StatusCode, body)
		}
		res, body = fx.do(t, http.MethodPost, base+"/error"+q, []byte(`{"code":"code_error","message":"forged"}`), jsonHeader)
		if res.StatusCode != http.StatusForbidden || string(body) != `{"error":"bad_token"}` {
			t.Errorf("error %q = %d %s", q, res.StatusCode, body)
		}
	}
	fx.mu.Lock()
	report, frames, errs := fx.report, len(fx.frames), len(fx.errors)
	fx.mu.Unlock()
	if report != nil || frames != 0 || errs != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("a hook ran: report %q, %d frames, %d errors", report, frames, errs)
	}
	// The one-shot stays free: the page's own report still reaches the hook.
	res, body := fx.do(t, http.MethodPost, base+"/report?t="+testRenderToken, []byte(`{"frame_count":3}`), jsonHeader)
	if res.StatusCode != http.StatusOK || !strings.Contains(string(body), `"start":true`) {
		t.Fatalf("report with the token = %d %s", res.StatusCode, body)
	}
}

func TestListenerKeepsThePrefixAndTheTokenOutOfAPageError(t *testing.T) {
	fx := newListenerFixture(t)
	base := fx.l.Base()
	// Chrome's text for a refused importScripts can name the URL, and the URL holds the prefix.
	sent, err := json.Marshal(PageError{Code: "code_error", Message: "refused " + base + "/scenes/s3.js?t=" + testRenderToken, SceneID: fx.l.prefix})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	res, body := fx.do(t, http.MethodPost, base+"/error?t="+testRenderToken, sent, map[string]string{"Content-Type": "application/json"})
	if res.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("error endpoint = %d %s", res.StatusCode, body)
	}
	fx.mu.Lock()
	defer fx.mu.Unlock()
	want := PageError{Code: "code_error", Message: "refused " + fx.l.Origin() + "//scenes/s3.js?t="}
	if len(fx.errors) != 1 || fx.errors[0] != want {
		t.Fatalf("errors seen = %+v, want %+v", fx.errors, want)
	}
}

func TestStartListenerRefusesAShortToken(t *testing.T) {
	prefix, err := NewPrefix()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	params := PageParams{Width: 1920, Height: 1080, FPS: 30, Transport: TransportPNG}
	for _, token := range []string{"", strings.Repeat("a", 31)} {
		params.Token = token
		if l, err := StartListener(prefix, &Snapshot{}, params, Hooks{}); err == nil {
			_ = l.Close()
			t.Errorf("token %q accepted", token)
		}
	}
	params.Token = testRenderToken
	l, err := StartListener(prefix, &Snapshot{}, params, Hooks{})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("a 32 character token was refused: %v", err)
	}
	_ = l.Close()
}
```

Still in `internal/storymaker/render/render_test.go`, add a page whose failures quote the prefix and the token, and a test of the three run paths that carry the page's text.

Old:
```go
func TestRunRefusesBadFrames(t *testing.T) {
```
New:
```go
// failingPage fails the way the real page does: Status gives __smStatus, WaitReady quotes its message, and a Capture
// error quotes the exception of __smRenderFrame, whose stack names the bundle URL under the base.
type failingPage struct {
	fakePage
	status     string
	readyErr   error
	captureErr error
}

func (p *failingPage) Status(context.Context) string   { return p.status }
func (p *failingPage) WaitReady(context.Context) error { return p.readyErr }
func (p *failingPage) Capture(context.Context, int) ([]byte, error) {
	return nil, p.captureErr
}

func TestRunKeepsThePrefixAndTheTokenOutOfAPageFailure(t *testing.T) {
	old := reportWait
	reportWait = time.Millisecond
	t.Cleanup(func() { reportWait = old })
	prefix, token := strings.Repeat("a", 32), strings.Repeat("b", 32)
	text := "boom at http://127.0.0.1:9/" + prefix + "/js/storymaker/host.bundle.js?t=" + token
	newRun := func() *renderRun {
		return &renderRun{prefix: prefix, token: token, frames: 1, st: Settings{TimeoutSeconds: 60}, failCh: make(chan *JobError, 1)}
	}
	check := func(name, got string) {
		t.Helper()
		if strings.Contains(got, prefix) || strings.Contains(got, token) || !strings.Contains(got, "boom") {
			t.Errorf("%s: %q", name, got)
		}
	}
	for name, page := range map[string]*failingPage{
		"ready":   {readyErr: errors.New(text)},
		"capture": {captureErr: errors.New(text)},
	} {
		run := newRun()
		run.driveScreenshots(context.Background(), page)
		select {
		case je := <-run.failCh:
			check(name, je.Message)
		default:
			t.Errorf("%s: no failure", name)
		}
	}
	// No report arrives, so wait ends with the report timeout, and that message quotes __smStatus.
	if err := newRun().wait(context.Background(), &failingPage{status: text}); err == nil {
		t.Error("status: no failure")
	} else {
		check("status", err.Error())
	}
}
```

New:
```js
check('render mode: the boot token goes on every POST and into no message', async () => {
    const token = 'feedfacefeedfacefeedfacefeedface', prefix = RBASE.slice(RBASE.lastIndexOf('/') + 1);
    const withToken = await runRender({ story: minimal, noCode: true, boot: { token } });
    assert.equal(withToken.status.phase, 'done', JSON.stringify(withToken.status));
    assert.ok(withToken.frames.length > 0);
    withToken.server.posts.forEach(p => assert.equal(new URL(p.url).searchParams.get('t'), token, p.url));
    const without = await runRender({ story: minimal, noCode: true });
    assert.equal(without.status.phase, 'done', JSON.stringify(without.status));
    without.server.posts.forEach(p => assert.equal(new URL(p.url).searchParams.has('t'), false, p.url));
    const refused = await runRender({ story: minimal, noCode: true, boot: { token }, reportStatus: 500 });
    assert.equal(refused.status.phase, 'error');
    const sent = refused.server.posts.find(p => p.path === '/error');
    assert.equal(new URL(sent.url).searchParams.get('t'), token, 'the failure report carries the token');
    assert.match(sent.error.message, /POST \/report answered 500/);
    const broken = await runRender({ bootStatus: 503 });
    const seen = [refused.status, sent.error, broken.status, broken.server.posts.find(p => p.path === '/error').error].map(v => JSON.stringify(v)).join('\n');
    assert.ok(!seen.includes(token), seen);
    assert.ok(!seen.includes(prefix), seen);
});
```

```go
func (l *Listener) report(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
```
New:
```go
// badTokenAnswer is the body of a 403. A wrong or missing token calls no hook and consumes nothing.
var badTokenAnswer = map[string]string{"error": "bad_token"}

// withoutSecrets removes the render prefix and the report token from a text the page wrote, before the text reaches the
// job record. A worker's error text can name a URL under the base, and the prefix in that URL is the read credential for
// the story and the catalog. Call it before clip, because a clip could leave a part of a secret. An empty secret changes
// nothing.
func withoutSecrets(text, prefix, token string) string {
	return strings.ReplaceAll(strings.ReplaceAll(text, prefix, ""), token, "")
}

// tokenOK compares the t query parameter with the job's token in constant time.
func (l *Listener) tokenOK(r *http.Request) bool {
	return subtle.ConstantTimeCompare([]byte(r.URL.Query().Get("t")), []byte(l.params.Token)) == 1
}

func (l *Listener) report(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
```

These lines stay as they are:
- The literal `post('/frame?n=' + n, body, { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain' })` at line 163. It must stay equal to the text in `internal/storymaker/render/browser_smoke_test.go`, or `TestGuardAndRequestOverheadSpike` silently stops preflighting. `browser_smoke_test.go` therefore needs no edit.
- The lines `status.message = H.asError(err).message;` and `await postError(err);`. The page's own failure text is clean now, because `H.fail` no longer gets a URL. A worker's `event.message` can still name a URL under the base, and `withoutSecrets` removes the prefix in Go before that text reaches the job record.

```go
func TestListenerServesFallbackFacesWithTheirType(t *testing.T) {
	prefix, err := NewPrefix()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	snap := &Snapshot{Fonts: []SnapshotFont{
		{Name: "default", Family: "Geist", Weight: 400, File: "/fonts/Geist-Regular.woff2", Data: []byte("woff2")},
		{Name: "sm-deva", Family: "SMDeva", Weight: 400, File: "/fonts/qr/NotoSansDevanagari.ttf", Data: []byte("ttf"), Fallback: true, WeightRange: "100 900"},
		{Name: "sm-cjk", Family: "SMCJK", Weight: 400, File: "/fonts/qr/NotoSansCJKsc.otf", Data: []byte("otf"), Fallback: true, WeightRange: "400", MaxBytes: 17 << 20},
		{Name: "sm-greek", Family: "SMGreek", Weight: 400, File: "/fonts/Greek.woff2", Data: []byte("greek"), Fallback: true, WeightRange: "300 700", UnicodeRange: "U+0370-03FF"},
	}}
	l, err := StartListener(prefix, snap, PageParams{Width: 1920, Height: 1080, FPS: 30, Transport: TransportPNG, Token: testRenderToken}, Hooks{})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = l.Close() })
	fx := &listenerFixture{l: l, client: &http.Client{Timeout: 5 * time.Second}}
	base := l.Base()

	_, body := fx.do(t, http.MethodGet, base+"/boot.json", nil, nil)
	var doc struct {
		Fonts []bootFont `json:"fonts"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(body, &doc); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := []bootFont{
		{Name: "default", Family: "Geist", Weight: 400, URL: base + "/fonts/Geist-Regular.woff2"},
		{Name: "sm-deva", Family: "SMDeva", Weight: 400, URL: base + "/fonts/qr/NotoSansDevanagari.ttf", Fallback: true, WeightRange: "100 900"},
		{Name: "sm-cjk", Family: "SMCJK", Weight: 400, URL: base + "/fonts/qr/NotoSansCJKsc.otf", Fallback: true, WeightRange: "400", MaxBytes: 17 << 20},
		{Name: "sm-greek", Family: "SMGreek", Weight: 400, URL: base + "/fonts/Greek.woff2", Fallback: true, WeightRange: "300 700", UnicodeRange: "U+0370-03FF"},
	}
	if len(doc.Fonts) != len(want) {
		t.Fatalf("boot.fonts = %+v", doc.Fonts)
	}
	for i := range want {
		if doc.Fonts[i] != want[i] {
			t.Errorf("boot.fonts[%d] = %+v, want %+v", i, doc.Fonts[i], want[i])
		}
	}
	for file, ct := range map[string]string{
		"/fonts/Geist-Regular.woff2":       "font/woff2",
		"/fonts/qr/NotoSansDevanagari.ttf": "font/ttf",
		"/fonts/qr/NotoSansCJKsc.otf":      "font/otf",
	} {
		res, _ := fx.do(t, http.MethodGet, base+file, nil, nil)
		if res.StatusCode != http.StatusOK || res.Header.Get("Content-Type") != ct {
			t.Errorf("GET %s = %d %q, want %q", file, res.StatusCode, res.Header.Get("Content-Type"), ct)
		}
	}
}

func TestSnapshotHoldsGlyphsAndFallbackFaces(t *testing.T) {
	ui := os.DirFS(uiDirForTests)
	cat, err := storymaker.LoadCatalog(ui, "img/storymaker/catalog.json")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	run := &renderRun{r: &Runner{deps: Deps{UI: ui, Catalog: cat}}, plan: &Plan{StoryJSON: []byte(`{}`)}}
	snap, err := run.snapshot()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(snap.CatalogFiles["glyphs.json"]) == 0 {
		t.Error("glyphs.json is not in the snapshot")
	}
	fallback := map[string]SnapshotFont{}
	for _, f := range snap.Fonts {
		if f.Fallback {
			fallback[f.Name] = f
		}
	}
	// 17 MiB is the one named exception to the 8 MiB cap (frozen section 6, Font caps).
	cjk, ok := fallback["sm-cjk"]
	if !ok || cjk.Family != "SMCJK" || cjk.Weight != 400 || cjk.WeightRange != "400" || cjk.MaxBytes != 17<<20 || cjk.File != "/fonts/qr/NotoSansCJKsc.otf" || len(cjk.Data) == 0 {
		t.Errorf("sm-cjk = %+v (ok %v, %d bytes)", cjk.Name, ok, len(cjk.Data))
	}
	greek, ok := fallback["sm-greek"]
	if !ok || greek.UnicodeRange != "U+0370-03FF" || greek.WeightRange != "300 700" || greek.Weight != 400 {
		t.Errorf("sm-greek = %q %q %d (ok %v)", greek.UnicodeRange, greek.WeightRange, greek.Weight, ok)
	}
	// Geist is a catalog font family, so its faces load through catalog.fonts only.
	if _, dup := fallback["geist"]; dup {
		t.Error("the geist face is listed as a fallback")
	}
}
```

New:
```js
const defaultFace = { name: 'default', family: 'Storymaker Default', weight: 400, url: RBASE + '/fonts/placeholder.woff2' };

check('render mode: fonts load on need through boot.fonts', async () => {
    const cjk = { name: 'sm-cjk', family: 'SMCJK', weight: 400, url: RBASE + '/fonts/qr/NotoSansCJKsc.otf', fallback: true, weight_range: '400', max_bytes: 64 };
    let pageEnv = null, loadMsg = null;
    const r = await runRender({
        boot: { fonts: [defaultFace, cjk] }, answer: () => ({ stop: true }),
        onEngine(env, engine) {
            pageEnv = env;
            const load = engine.load;
            engine.load = msg => { loadMsg = msg; return load.call(engine, msg); };
        }
    });
    assert.equal(r.status.phase, 'stopped', JSON.stringify(r.status));
    assert.deepEqual(plain(loadMsg.fonts), [], 'load.fonts stays empty');
    assert.equal(r.server.gets.filter(u => u === defaultFace.url).length, 1, 'the face the story needs is fetched');
    assert.ok(!r.server.gets.includes(cjk.url), 'a Latin story fetches no CJK face');
    // The mini catalog has no glyph data, so compile never asks for this key. Ask the page's asset source directly.
    const got = await pageEnv.requestAssets(['font:sm-cjk/400', 'font:sm-arab/400']);
    assert.equal(got.assets['font:sm-cjk/400'].byteLength, 16);   // the stub answers 16 bytes, inside max_bytes 64
    assert.deepEqual(plain(got.missing), ['font:sm-arab/400'], 'a key outside boot.fonts is missing');
    await pageEnv.requestAssets(['font:sm-cjk/400']);
    assert.equal(r.server.gets.filter(u => u === cjk.url).length, 1, 'the buffer is cached per key');
});

check('render mode: a font over its limit is a readiness failure', async () => {
    // The stub answers 16 bytes, which is over max_bytes 8.
    const named = await runRender({ boot: { fonts: [{ ...defaultFace, max_bytes: 8 }] } });
    assert.equal(named.status.phase, 'error', JSON.stringify(named.status));
    assert.equal(named.status.error, 'readiness');
    assert.equal(named.server.posts.filter(p => p.path === '/report').length, 0, 'fonts load before the report');
    assert.equal(named.server.posts.find(p => p.path === '/error').error.code, 'readiness');
    // Without max_bytes the limit is 8 MiB, so one byte more fails.
    const byDefault = await runRender({ fontBytes: 8 * 1048576 + 1 });
    assert.equal(byDefault.status.phase, 'error', JSON.stringify(byDefault.status));
    assert.equal(byDefault.status.error, 'readiness');
});

check('render mode: catalog.glyphs is fetched into catalog.glyph_data', async () => {
    const glyphData = { schema: 'storymaker-glyphs/1', faces: [] };
    let loadMsg = null;
    const r = await runRender({
        story: minimal, noCode: true, answer: () => ({ stop: true }),
        catalogFiles: { 'catalog.json': { ...t.catalog, glyphs: { file: 'glyphs.json' } }, 'glyphs.json': glyphData },
        onEngine(env, engine) {
            const load = engine.load;
            engine.load = msg => { loadMsg = msg; return load.call(engine, msg); };
        }
    });
    assert.equal(r.status.phase, 'stopped', JSON.stringify(r.status));
    assert.ok(r.server.gets.includes(RBASE + '/catalog/glyphs.json'));
    assert.deepEqual(plain(loadMsg.catalog.glyph_data), glyphData);
});
```

```go
// fontContentType picks the type by extension. The Noto fallback faces are TTF and OTF.
func fontContentType(file string) string {
	switch strings.ToLower(path.Ext(file)) {
	case ".ttf":
		return "font/ttf"
	case ".otf":
		return "font/otf"
	}
	return "font/woff2"
}
```

New:
```go
	readFont := func(file string) ([]byte, error) {
		clean := strings.TrimPrefix(path.Clean(file), "/")
		if !strings.HasPrefix(file, "/fonts/") || !fs.ValidPath(clean) {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("the catalog font file %q is not a /fonts/ path", file)
		}
		return read(clean)
	}
	catalogFamilies := map[string]bool{}
	for _, f := range cat.Fonts {
		data, err := readFont(f.File)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		catalogFamilies[f.Family] = true
		snap.Fonts = append(snap.Fonts, SnapshotFont{Name: f.Name, Family: f.Family, Weight: f.Weight, File: f.File, Data: data})
	}
	// Every fallback face is offered, and the page fetches one only when compile needs it. A face whose
	// family is a catalog font loads through catalog.fonts, so it is not listed twice.
	if glyphs := cat.Glyphs(); glyphs != nil {
		for _, face := range glyphs.Faces {
			if catalogFamilies[face.Family] {
				continue
			}
			data, err := readFont(face.File)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			snap.Fonts = append(snap.Fonts, SnapshotFont{
				Name: face.ID, Family: face.Family, Weight: 400, File: face.File, Data: data,
				Fallback: true, UnicodeRange: face.UnicodeRange, WeightRange: face.Weight, MaxBytes: face.MaxBytes,
			})
		}
	}
	return snap, nil
```

Old:

New:
```js
            var assetsBase = String(boot.assets_base || (base + '/catalog/')).replace(/\/*$/, '/');
            if (catalog && catalog.glyphs && catalog.glyphs.file) {
                var glyphFile = String(catalog.glyphs.file);
                if (glyphFile.indexOf('..') >= 0 || glyphFile.charAt(0) === '/') throw H.fail('readiness', 'Bad asset path ' + glyphFile);
                catalog.glyph_data = await getJSON('glyphs', assetsBase + glyphFile);
            }
            // Fonts are lazy: load.fonts stays empty and requestAssets fetches a face when compile needs it. URLs come from boot.fonts
            // only (the catalog's file paths are AuraGo paths), and each buffer is fetched once.
            var fontByKey = {}, fontFetches = {};
            (boot.fonts || []).forEach(function (f) { fontByKey['font:' + f.name + '/' + f.weight] = f; });
            function fetchFont(key) {
                if (!fontFetches[key]) {
                    fontFetches[key] = (async function () {
                        var f = fontByKey[key], res = await doFetch(f.url, { cache: 'no-store' });
                        if (!res.ok) throw H.fail('readiness', 'Font "' + f.family + '" could not be fetched (' + res.status + ').');
                        var data = await res.arrayBuffer(), limit = f.max_bytes > 0 ? f.max_bytes : MAX_FONT_BYTES;
                        if (data.byteLength > limit) throw H.fail('readiness', 'Font "' + f.family + '" has ' + data.byteLength + ' bytes, over its limit of ' + limit + '.');
                        return data;
                    })();
                }
                return fontFetches[key];
            }
```

Old:
```js
            env.requestAssets = async function (keys) {
                var assets = {};
```

New:
```js
            env.requestAssets = async function (keys) {
                var assets = {}, missing = [];
                await Promise.all(keys.map(async function (key) {
                    if (key.indexOf('catalog:') === 0) {
                        var rel = key.slice(8);
                        if (rel.indexOf('..') >= 0 || rel.charAt(0) === '/') throw H.fail('readiness', 'Bad asset path ' + rel);
                        var res = await doFetch(assetsBase + rel, { cache: 'no-store' });
                        if (!res.ok) throw H.fail('readiness', 'Asset ' + rel + ' answered ' + res.status);
                        assets[key] = await res.json();
                    } else if (key.indexOf('font:') === 0) {
                        if (fontByKey[key]) assets[key] = await fetchFont(key); else missing.push(key);
                    }
                }));
                return { assets: assets, missing: missing };
            };
```

```go
// seedPieces stores audio and a valid envelope for every piece of blocks, as a finished narrate job leaves them.
func seedPieces(t *testing.T, fx *runFixture, blocks []storymaker.NarrationBlock) {
	t.Helper()
	samples := make([]float32, 24000) // 1 s at 24 kHz
	for i := range samples {
		samples[i] = 0.1 // -20 dBFS, above the speech threshold of -40 dBFS
	}
	env := storymaker.ComputeEnvelope(storymaker.DecodedAudio{Rate: 24000, Channels: 1, Samples: samples})
	for _, b := range blocks {
		for _, p := range storymaker.PiecesOf(b) {
			if err := fx.projects.WritePiece(context.Background(), fx.projectID, storymaker.PieceKey(b.Voice, p.Text), "wav", []byte("RIFF"), env); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
		}
	}
}

// bootOfJob runs plan as a job, returns the boot document the page reads, and cancels the job.
func bootOfJob(t *testing.T, fx *runFixture, plan *Plan) []byte {
	t.Helper()
	got := make(chan []byte, 1)
	fx.page.script = func(ctx context.Context, base string) {
		if data, _, err := httpDo(ctx, "GET", base+"/boot.json", "", nil, nil); err == nil {
			got <- data
		}
	}
	job, _, err := fx.mgr.Enqueue(EnqueueRequest{ProjectID: fx.projectID, Payload: plan})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer func() {
		fx.mgr.Cancel(job.ID)
		waitJob(t, fx.mgr, job.ID, terminal)
	}()
	select {
	case data := <-got:
		return data
	case <-time.After(time.Minute):
		t.Fatal("the page never read boot.json")
		return nil
	}
}

func TestBootCarriesThePlanAudioInfo(t *testing.T) {
	fx := newRunFixture(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	if _, err := fx.projects.SaveStory(ctx, fx.projectID, []byte(narratedStoryJSON), ""); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	unsynthesised, err := fx.runner.Prepare(ctx, fx.projectID, "", fx.settings)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if string(unsynthesised.AudioJSON) != `{"narration":{}}` {
		t.Errorf("AudioJSON without audio = %s", unsynthesised.AudioJSON)
	}
	if len(unsynthesised.Blocks) != 1 || unsynthesised.Blocks[0].ID != "s1:n" {
		t.Fatalf("Blocks = %+v", unsynthesised.Blocks)
	}
	seedPieces(t, fx, unsynthesised.Blocks)

	plan, err := fx.runner.Prepare(ctx, fx.projectID, "", fx.settings)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// Two sentences of 7 words each: neither merges, so the block has two pieces.
	if blocks := plan.AudioInfo.Narration["s1"].Blocks; len(blocks) != 1 || len(blocks[0].Pieces) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("AudioInfo = %+v", plan.AudioInfo)
	}
	var doc struct {
		AudioInfo struct {
			Narration json.RawMessage `json:"narration"`
		} `json:"audio_info"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(bootOfJob(t, fx, plan), &doc); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// GET /narration encodes AudioAnswer with encoding/json, so this is the narration value of that route.
	answer, err := fx.projects.AudioAnswer(ctx, fx.projectID, &plan.Story, nil)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want, err := json.Marshal(answer.Narration)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if string(doc.AudioInfo.Narration) != string(want) {
		t.Errorf("boot.audio_info.narration:\n%s\nwant:\n%s", doc.AudioInfo.Narration, want)
	}
}
```

```go
func TestEgressGuardDecisions(t *testing.T) {
```

New:
```go
var (
	pieceHashA = strings.Repeat("a", 64)
	pieceHashB = strings.Repeat("b", 64)
	pieceHashC = strings.Repeat("c", 64)
	pieceHashD = strings.Repeat("d", 64)
)

// measuredReport is goodReport with a three-piece narration in s1 and a one-piece dialogue in s2.
// goodReport has 300 frames at 30 fps, so filmMS is round(300*1000/30) = 10000.
func measuredReport() map[string]any {
	m := goodReport()
	m["audio_events"] = []any{
		map[string]any{"kind": "narration", "ref": pieceHashA, "start_ms": 500, "scene_id": "s1", "block_id": "s1:n", "piece": 0},
		map[string]any{"kind": "narration", "ref": pieceHashB, "start_ms": 1500, "scene_id": "s1", "block_id": "s1:n", "piece": 1},
		map[string]any{"kind": "narration", "ref": pieceHashC, "start_ms": 2500, "scene_id": "s1", "block_id": "s1:n", "piece": 2},
		map[string]any{"kind": "dialogue", "ref": pieceHashD, "start_ms": 5000, "scene_id": "s2", "block_id": "s2:b1", "piece": 0},
	}
	m["captions"] = []any{
		map[string]any{"start_ms": 500, "end_ms": 1500, "lines": []any{"Der erste Satz."}, "scene_id": "s1", "block_id": "s1:n"},
		map[string]any{"start_ms": 1500, "end_ms": 3500, "lines": []any{"Der zweite Satz.", "Der dritte"}, "scene_id": "s1", "block_id": "s1:n"},
	}
	return m
}

func measuredExpect() reportExpect {
	want := reportExpectation()
	want.Blocks = map[string]reportBlock{
		"s1:n":  {SceneID: "s1", Kind: "narration", Hashes: []string{pieceHashA, pieceHashB, pieceHashC}, Text: captionKey("Der erste Satz. Der zweite Satz. Der dritte Satz.")},
		"s2:b1": {SceneID: "s2", Kind: "dialogue", Hashes: []string{pieceHashD}, Text: captionKey("Hallo!")},
	}
	want.MaxEvents = 4 // three pieces of s1:n plus one of s2:b1
	return want
}

func measuredEvents(m map[string]any) []any   { return m["audio_events"].([]any) }
func measuredCaptions(m map[string]any) []any { return m["captions"].([]any) }
func measuredEvent(m map[string]any, i int) map[string]any {
	return measuredEvents(m)[i].(map[string]any)
}
func measuredCaption(m map[string]any, i int) map[string]any {
	return measuredCaptions(m)[i].(map[string]any)
}

func TestParseReportRefusesBadAudioEventsAndCaptions(t *testing.T) {
	cases := []struct {
		name string
		mut  func(m map[string]any)
		text string
	}{
		{"unknown block", func(m map[string]any) { measuredEvent(m, 0)["block_id"] = "s9:n" }, "unknown block"},
		{"wrong hash", func(m map[string]any) { measuredEvent(m, 1)["ref"] = pieceHashA }, "does not match"},
		{"duplicate piece", func(m map[string]any) {
			measuredEvent(m, 1)["piece"] = 0
			measuredEvent(m, 1)["ref"] = pieceHashA
		}, "twice"},
		{"gap in the piece prefix", func(m map[string]any) {
			e := measuredEvents(m)
			m["audio_events"] = []any{e[0], e[2], e[3]}
		}, "gap"},
		{"a narration block with no event", func(m map[string]any) { m["audio_events"] = measuredEvents(m)[3:] }, "no audio event"},
		{"empty audio_events with measured blocks", func(m map[string]any) { m["audio_events"] = []any{} }, "no audio event"},
		{"unknown kind", func(m map[string]any) { measuredEvent(m, 0)["kind"] = "music" }, "unknown kind"},
		// filmMS is 10000 and the bound is strict.
		{"a time at the film end", func(m map[string]any) { measuredEvent(m, 3)["start_ms"] = 10000 }, "outside the film"},
		// |10002-10000| = 2, over the tolerance of 1.
		{"duration_ms off by 2", func(m map[string]any) { m["duration_ms"] = 10002 }, "duration_ms"},
		{"over MaxEvents", func(m map[string]any) {
			m["audio_events"] = append(measuredEvents(m), map[string]any{"kind": "narration", "ref": pieceHashA, "start_ms": 600, "scene_id": "s1", "block_id": "s1:n", "piece": 0})
		}, "too many"},
		{"over 8000 captions", func(m map[string]any) {
			caps := make([]any, maxReportCaptions+1)
			for i := range caps {
				caps[i] = measuredCaption(m, 0)
			}
			m["captions"] = caps
		}, "8001 captions"},
		{"a caption with 0 lines", func(m map[string]any) { measuredCaption(m, 0)["lines"] = []any{} }, "1 or 2 lines"},
		{"an empty line", func(m map[string]any) { measuredCaption(m, 0)["lines"] = []any{" \t"} }, "empty line"},
		// 257 UTF-16 units, one over the cap.
		{"a long line", func(m map[string]any) { measuredCaption(m, 0)["lines"] = []any{strings.Repeat("a", 257)} }, "256"},
		{"a caption not in its block", func(m map[string]any) { measuredCaption(m, 0)["lines"] = []any{"Ein fremder Satz."} }, "not in the text"},
		{"a caption of an unknown block", func(m map[string]any) { measuredCaption(m, 0)["block_id"] = "s9:n" }, "unknown block"},
		{"a caption past the film", func(m map[string]any) { measuredCaption(m, 1)["end_ms"] = 10001 }, "outside the film"},
	}
	for _, tc := range cases {
		m := measuredReport()
		tc.mut(m)
		body, _ := json.Marshal(m)
		_, refusal := parseReport(body, measuredExpect())
		if refusal == nil || refusal.Code != CodeRenderFailed || !strings.Contains(refusal.Message, tc.text) {
			t.Errorf("%s: refusal = %+v", tc.name, refusal)
		}
	}
}

func TestParseReportAcceptsHonestAudioReports(t *testing.T) {
	accept := func(name string, want reportExpect, mut func(m map[string]any)) compileReport {
		t.Helper()
		m := measuredReport()
		if mut != nil {
			mut(m)
		}
		body, _ := json.Marshal(m)
		rep, refusal := parseReport(body, want)
		if refusal != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: refused: %v", name, refusal)
		}
		return rep
	}
	if rep := accept("the full report", measuredExpect(), nil); len(rep.AudioEvents) != 4 || len(rep.Captions) != 2 || rep.AudioEvents[3].BlockID != "s2:b1" {
		t.Errorf("rep = %+v", rep)
	}
	accept("duration_ms off by 1", measuredExpect(), func(m map[string]any) { m["duration_ms"] = 10001 })
	accept("a dialogue block with no event", measuredExpect(), func(m map[string]any) { m["audio_events"] = measuredEvents(m)[:3] })
	accept("a film end that dropped the last piece", measuredExpect(), func(m map[string]any) {
		e := measuredEvents(m)
		m["audio_events"] = []any{e[0], e[1], e[3]}
	})
	accept("a JS gain", measuredExpect(), func(m map[string]any) { measuredEvent(m, 0)["gain"] = 99 })

	withControl := measuredExpect()
	block := withControl.Blocks["s1:n"]
	block.Text = captionKey("Der erste\x01 Satz. Der zweite Satz. Der dritte Satz.")
	withControl.Blocks["s1:n"] = block
	accept("a narration with U+0001", withControl, func(m map[string]any) {
		measuredCaption(m, 0)["lines"] = []any{"Der erste\x01 Satz."}
	})

	estimated := measuredExpect()
	estimated.CaptionText = map[string]string{"s3:n": captionKey("Noch ohne Audio.")}
	accept("a caption of an estimated block", estimated, func(m map[string]any) {
		m["captions"] = append(measuredCaptions(m), map[string]any{"start_ms": 8000, "end_ms": 9000, "lines": []any{"Noch ohne Audio."}, "scene_id": "s3", "block_id": "s3:n"})
	})
	// An estimated block still takes no audio event.
	m := measuredReport()
	event := measuredEvent(m, 3)
	event["kind"], event["block_id"], event["scene_id"] = "narration", "s3:n", "s3"
	body, _ := json.Marshal(m)
	if _, refusal := parseReport(body, estimated); refusal == nil || !strings.Contains(refusal.Message, "unknown block") {
		t.Errorf("an event of an estimated block: %+v", refusal)
	}
}

func TestCaptionKeyRemovesJSSpaceAndControls(t *testing.T) {
	// \xc2\xa0 is U+00A0 and \xe3\x80\x80 is U+3000, both JS whitespace.
	if got := captionKey(" Der\xc2\xa0erste\x01\tSatz\xe3\x80\x80. "); got != "DerersteSatz." {
		t.Errorf("captionKey = %q", got)
	}
}

// The plan's own blocks reach parseReport: a report without events cannot defeat a measured narration.
```

In `internal/storymaker/render/report.go`, make these replacements.

Old:
```go
import (
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
```
